An automatic terrain-annotation pipeline recovers full 3D contact geometry from retargeted motion, enabling terrain annotation at the scale of existing motion datasets. The predicted distribution is reused at deployment to detect implausible commands online and retract them onto learned behaviors, so the robot attempts tasks in a "best-effort" manner. The result is a unified policy that takes real-time command, interacts with environment, and stays robust to implausible commands, falls, and disturbances. GigaBrain-WBC-0.5 achieves the highest success rate across all four regimes among three large-scale tracker baselines: 81.3% on terrain interaction (4.3x the strongest baseline), 83.1% under implausible commands, and 99.3% fall recovery (16.8x the strongest baseline). Hardware trials show robust interaction under missing supports and disturbances; the Unitree G1 checkpoint transfers to the Maker L01 robot with simple fine-tuning.

</details>


### [262] [GAPL: Grounded Action-effect Policy Learning for LLM-Based Trajectory Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18254)
*Zhihong Cui,Hengyu Liu,Zhangkai Wu,Yushuai Li,Tianyi Li,Peiyuan Guan,Amir Taherkordi,Tor Skeie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GAPL框架，将大语言模型的效应估计与仿真驱动的效应接地相结合，通过PPO策略优化实现自动驾驶轨迹规划，在高速路场景中显著降低碰撞率与位移误差并提升奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶轨迹规划需要高层推理与低层控制协同，LLM虽具有语义丰富的规划能力，但存在幻觉推理、缺乏环境动力学接地以及数值控制精度不足等问题，因此需要统一框架来弥补这些缺陷。

Method: GAPL由三个模块组成：(1)基于LLM的效应评估器，用于结构化多维动作效应估计；(2)基于仿真的效应接地器，通过模拟器rollout预测动力学一致的效应；(3)效应感知决策器，利用蒸馏器将LLM效应估计与仿真接地对比，引导基于PPO的策略学习，形成闭环系统。

Result: 在四个Highway-env场景上的实验表明，GAPL一致优于基线方法，平均碰撞率降低0.76，平均位移误差(ADE)降低0.86，最终位移误差(FDE)降低2.00，平均奖励提升1.44。

Conclusion: 通过将LLM的推理能力与仿真的物理一致性相结合，GAPL有效解决了LLM在自动驾驶规划中的幻觉与数值精度问题，验证了语言模型与仿真接地策略学习协同的可行性。

Abstract: Trajectory planning for autonomous driving requires both high-level reasoning and precise low-level control. Large Language Models (LLMs) offer semantic-rich planning capabilities, however, their application is limited by hallucinated reasoning, poor grounding in environment dynamics, and limited numerical precision in control. We propose GAPL (Grounded Action-effect Policy Learning), a unified framework that integrates LLM-based effect estimation, simulation-based effect grounding, and policy optimization into a closed-loop system. GAPL consists of three modules: (1) an LLM-based Effect Evaluator for structured multi-dimensional action-effect estimation; (2) a Simulation-based Effect Grounder that predicts dynamics-consistent effects from simulator rollouts; and (3) an Effect-Aware Decision Maker that grounds LLM effect estimates against simulation via a distiller to guide Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO)-based policy learning. Experiments on four Highway-env scenarios demonstrate that GAPL consistently outperforms baselines, achieving average reductions of {0.76, 0.86, 2.00} in collision rate, average displacement error (ADE), and final displacement error (FDE), and an average reward gain of 1.44.

</details>


### [263] [VERAGMIL: Virtual Environment for Scooping Granular Foods with Imitation Learning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18258)
*Amanuel Ergogo,Diego Dall'Alba,Przemyslaw Korzeniowski*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出VERAGMIL框架，结合高保真模拟器与虚拟现实(VR)界面，用于机器人辅助喂食系统中颗粒状食物（如米饭、豆类）的操作任务演示采集与模仿学习训练。通过BC、BC-RNN、BCQ三种模仿学习模型对比评估，发现VR演示显著优于3D空间鼠标，其中BCQ表现最佳，接近人类水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人辅助喂食系统中，操作颗粒状食物（如米饭、豆类）因具有动态物理特性而极具挑战性。从人类演示中学习是可行方案，但获取高质量的演示数据十分复杂，现有方法缺乏有效的演示采集工具和训练环境。

Method: 提出VERAGMIL框架：(1) 构建高保真模拟器环境，包含机器人、传感器及多种具有不同物理特性的食物模型，用于颗粒材料操作训练；(2) 设计直观的VR界面用于记录演示数据，支持多种模仿学习方法；(3) 在颗粒状食物的舀取和搬运任务上训练三种模仿学习模型（BC、BC-RNN、BCQ），并使用VR界面和3D空间鼠标两种输入方式采集演示，与人类专家基线进行对比评估。

Result: VR-based演示数据显著优于3D空间鼠标采集的数据；BCQ在所有模型中表现最佳，尤其在减少食物洒落方面效果突出，整体性能接近人类专家水平。模型在成功率、洒落量、可泛化性（对未见食物）和任务完成时间等指标上进行了全面评估。

Conclusion: VERAGMIL框架有效支撑了机器人辅助喂食系统在颗粒材料处理任务上的训练，验证了VR演示采集方式与BCQ方法相结合的有效性，代码已公开。

Abstract: Robot-Assisted Feeding (RAF) systems are essential for assisting individuals with disabilities or motor impairments in eating tasks. Manipulating granular food items, such as rice and beans, poses significant challenges due to their dynamic physical properties. Learning from human demonstrations offers a promising solution, but acquiring high-quality demonstrations is complex. To address this, we present VERAGMIL, a framework that combines a high-fidelity simulator with an intuitive Virtual Reality (VR) interface for recording demonstrations and supporting different imitation learning methods. VERAGMIL provides a realistic environment for training RAF systems to handle granular materials, including robots, sensors, and various food items with distinct physical characteristics. We evaluate VERAGMIL by training three imitation learning models, BC, BC-RNN, and BCQ, on granular scooping and transporting tasks using both VR interface and 3D space mouse demonstrations, comparing them with a human-expert baseline. The models are assessed on success rate, spillage, generalization to unseen food items, and task completion time. Results show that VR-based demonstrations significantly outperform 3D space mouse data, with BCQ achieving the best overall performance, particularly in reducing spillage and approaching human performance. These findings underscore the effectiveness of our framework for training RAF systems in granular material handling. The code for our framework is publicly available at: https://github.com/AmanuelErgogo/VERAGMIL.git.

</details>


### [264] [Transferable Tool-Tissue Contact Detection from Stereo Depth in Robot-Assisted Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18270)
*Mingyeung Wu,Zhonghao Zhang,Hao Yang,Alan Kuntz,Jie Ying Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出利用深度图像中工具-组织之间的最小距离信号（-log10|d|）进行机器人辅助手术中的器械-组织接触检测，通过全监督隐马尔可夫模型（HMM）建模，在跨任务、跨模型的测试中达到宏F1 0.927和AUPRC 0.980，显著优于基于RGB外观的接触分类器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的器械-组织接触检测可以支持交互感知控制和下游力估计，但现有方法大多基于RGB外观学习接触分类器，泛化能力差。作者希望通过利用深度图像提供的几何信息（距离）来提升接触检测的跨器械、跨任务的迁移能力。

Method: 从立体视觉生成的深度图像中，在工具边界周围定位一个空间支持的最小距离patch，并将其压缩为标量信号-log10|d|。该信号随接触状态变化而升降。将该观测形式化建模为全监督的两状态隐马尔可夫模型（HMM），采用六折留一会话（LOSO）集成方式在六个触诊会话上训练，决策阈值从池化的折叠外预测中选择。

Result: 在四个留出会话上评估：同任务同模型、同任务不同模型、不同任务不同模型三类场景。模型达到宏F1 0.927和AUPRC 0.980。对比的RGB基线模型在第一类场景表现良好（F1 0.965），但在另两类场景大幅下降，宏F1仅0.320。

Conclusion: 器械-组织距离是接触检测中一个强且可迁移的线索，基于深度图像的几何信号相比RGB外观特征具有显著更好的泛化能力，适用于机器人辅助手术中的接触检测任务。

Abstract: Reliable tool--tissue contact detection can support interaction-aware control and downstream force estimation in robot-assisted surgery. Most existing methods learn a contact classifier from RGB appearance, which is hard to generalize. In this work, we use the depth image generated from a stereo pair to give more information about tool--tissue contact. For each depth frame, we localize a spatially supported minimum-distance patch around the tool boundary and reduce it to a single scalar, $-\log_{10}|d|$; this signal rises and falls in step with ground-truth contact. We formalize this observation with a fully supervised two-state hidden Markov model. We fit this model as a six-fold leave-one-session-out (LOSO) ensemble on six palpation sessions against a single silicone cup-like phantom, with the decision threshold selected from the pooled out-of-fold predictions. It is evaluated on four held-out sessions of three categories: 1. same task on same phantom; 2. same task on different phantom; 3. different task on different phantom. This model reaches held-out macro F1 $0.927$ and AUPRC $0.980$. We further compare against a reproduction of an RGB-based contact classifier from prior work. This RGB-based model achieves high performance on the first category (F1 $0.965$), but substantially lower performance on the other two, resulting in macro F1 $0.320$ across all four sessions. These results indicate that the tool--tissue distance is a strong, transferable cue for contact detection in robot-assisted surgery.

</details>


### [265] [GuideFetch: A Task Coordination Framework for Concurrent Navigation and Object Retrieval in Assistive Robot Dogs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18292)
*Qian Yin,Ruiping Liu,Kunyu Peng,Jianxiang Man,Isik Baran Sandan,Junwei Zheng,Yufan Chen,Di Wen,Kailun Yang,Rainer Stiefelhagen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出GuideFetch，一个用于异构引导者-取物者机器人团队并发执行导航与物体取回任务的协调框架。LLM根据自然语言指令实例化基于调度条件的四动作模式，执行前进行别名归一化与有效性验证，执行时依据机器人状态进行串行/并行调度。在90种场景组合（360次执行）的2×2匹配研究中，所有在线LLM响应均无需回退或重放即通过验证，并行执行相较串行将makespan缩短41.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人导盲犬引导盲人就座的同时，另一只辅助机器狗去取咖啡并送达同一座位，这一场景天然需要并发执行。然而语法有效的LLM规划可能违反具身约束，且控制器表现出成功动作并不能确证任务完成。现有的LLM规划方法缺乏对异构机器人团队并发执行的有效协调，且无法区分计划有效性与任务实际完成状态。

Method: GuideFetch框架包含三个主要阶段：(1) LLM从自然语言指令实例化一个调度条件化的四动作模式；(2) 执行前归一化机器人、技能和目标别名，并对照注册目标、机器人能力及所选调度对动作进行验证；(3) 根据机器人目标状态决定串行与并行执行。通过匹配的2×2研究设计（LLM vs 脚本规划源 × 串行 vs 并行调度），在90种场景与种子组合共360次执行中进行系统评估。

Result: 在360次执行中，所有180个在线LLM响应均通过验证（无需回退或重放）并与对应脚本计划匹配。每个规划源下，串行与并行执行分别达到72/90和71/90次操作成功率。在两种调度均完成的56个案例中，并行执行将平均makespan缩短41.3%。

Conclusion: 在受控设定下，角色专业化与动作重叠可缩短完成任务的时间，而状态检查能够区分计划有效性与已验证的任务完成。GuideFetch证明了LLM生成的并发多机器人计划可以通过验证与状态驱动执行来保证可行性与可完成性，为异构机器人团队协调提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Consider a robot guide dog escorting a blind user to an available seat while a second assistive robot dog concurrently retrieves a cup of coffee and delivers it to the same seat. This setting motivates concurrent execution because navigation and object retrieval can overlap. A syntactically valid Large Language Model (LLM) plan may still violate embodiment constraints, and successful-looking controller motion does not by itself establish task completion. We introduce \textsc{GuideFetch}, a coordination framework for concurrent navigation and object retrieval by a heterogeneous guider and fetcher team. An LLM instantiates a schedule-conditioned four-action schema from a natural-language instruction. Before execution, robot, skill, and target aliases are normalized, and proposed actions are validated against registered targets, robot capabilities, and the selected schedule. Robot and object states then govern sequential and parallel execution. In a matched $2\times2$ study across 90 combinations of scene and seed (360 executions), all 180 online LLM responses validate without fallback or replay and match the corresponding scripted plans. For each planner source, sequential and parallel execution achieve $72/90$ and $71/90$ operational successes, respectively. Among the 56 cases completed by both schedules, parallel execution reduces mean makespan by 41.3\%. Within this controlled setting, role specialization and action overlap shorten completed missions, while state checks distinguish plan validity from verified mission completion. Source code will be available.

</details>


### [266] [A Task-Agnostic Control Strategy for Dynamic Assistance with Pneumatically Actuated Soft Exosuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18364)
*Anoush Sepehri,Zachary Huang,Raymond de Callafon,Michael T. Tolley,Tania K. Morimoto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向气动软体外骨骼的逆对象控制策略，仅依赖运动学传感即可提供任务无关的动态辅助。通过Hammerstein动态模型（结合Preisach磁滞模型和线性时不变滤波器）对系统建模，并用140秒数据进行个性化，最终在手腕软体外骨骼测试台上将交互力矩降低73%，关键屈肌/伸肌激活降低47%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气动人工肌肉为上肢软体外骨骼在康复、增力和日常生活辅助中提供了新机遇，但其复杂动力学和有限带宽使得基于用户意图的响应式辅助成为长期挑战。现有方法通常需要生理传感器或力传感器来解析用户意图，复杂且不便携。

Method: 采用Hammerstein动态模型对人与机器人耦合系统建模，该模型由Preisach磁滞模型（捕捉静态非线性/迟滞行为）和线性时不变滤波器（捕捉动态行为）级联组成。使用140秒数据进行用户个性化建模，并在控制环路中近似求逆以补偿系统动力学，从而在仅依赖运动学传感的条件下实现任务无关的动态辅助。

Result: 在模拟手腕软辅助外骨骼的测试台上评估，控制器在8°/s至120°/s的速度范围内，将交互力矩最多降低73%，关键屈肌和伸肌的激活最多降低47%（相对无辅助条件）。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种无需生理或力传感器即可解析用户意图的控制策略，可为气动软体外骨骼提供任务无关、动态的辅助，拓展了软体外骨骼在日常生活中的应用可行性。

Abstract: Pneumatic artificial muscles have provided new opportunities to develop upper-extremity soft exosuits for reha- bilitation, augmentation, and assisted daily living. However, the complex dynamics and limited bandwidth of these actuators has made providing responsive assistance based on user intention a longstanding challenge. In this work, we present an inverse-plant control strategy for pneumatically actuated soft exosuits that only relies on kinematic sensing for task-agnostic and dynamic assistance during daily living. We model the human-robot system using a Hammerstein dynamic model, consisting of a Preisach hysteresis model and a linear time-invariant filter, to capture the static and dynamic behavior of the system. We personalize our model to each user using 140 s of data and approximate an inverse to integrate into our control loop. When evaluated on a test rig that emulated a soft assistive exosuit for the wrist, our controller reduced the interaction torque by up to 73% and the activation of key flexor and extensor muscles by up to 47% relative to the condition with no assistance for speeds ranging from 8°/s to 120°/s. Overall, this work presents a control strategy that can provide task-agnostic, dynamic assistance with pneumatically actuated soft exosuits without the need for physiological or force sensors to interpret user intention.

</details>


### [267] [The Embodiment Gap in Robot Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18433)
*Yukiyasu Domae,Keisuke Shirai,Hanbit Oh,Ryoichi Nakajo,Tomohiro Motoda,Koshi Makihara,Masaki Murooka,Takuma Yagi,Yoshiaki Bando,Ryo Hanai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"具身差距"（embodiment gap）概念，指可复用模型/表征/数据与实际部署到特定机器人上执行之间所需的工作量差距。文章系统梳理了跨具身可复用与需重新实现的内容，提出二维映射图和研究方向分类，并设计了不以成功率论英雄的报告框架，用于评估跨具身学习中的适配成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人基础模型（如VLA策略）常被视为可扩展模型，但不同方法在不同机器人上的部署适配工作量差异巨大，而现有讨论多从数据量、模型规模的角度出发，忽视了从"可复用模型"到"实际在目标机器人上执行"之间的适配成本，这一差异直接影响实际部署决策。

Method: 作者提出"具身差距"这一核心概念，将现有方法置于二维映射图中：一个维度是共享结构的类型（共享什么），另一维度是需要适配的阶段（何时对目标机器人执行进行适配）。随后通过三个重叠的研究方向梳理相关文献：共享语义与感知、共享机器人数据与接口、跨具身对应关系学习。最后提出一个报告框架，用于识别跨具身学习中应检查的工作量以及在新机器人上仍需完成的工作。

Result: 本文提供了一套系统化的分析框架，将跨具身学习研究进行归类，并揭示了仅以成功率衡量跨具身泛化能力的不足。所提报告框架帮助研究者明确在比较跨具身学习方法时需要检查哪些适配工作，并识别后续仍需解决的研究问题。

Conclusion: 跨具身学习中"能泛化"与"能部署"是两回事。研究者应当显式报告适配成本，而非仅依靠成功率来评估跨具身方法。具身差距的概念为未来跨具身学习提供了更全面、更具实操导向的评估视角。

Abstract: Robot foundation models (RFMs), including vision-language-action (VLA) policies, are often discussed through a scaling view: more data, larger models, and broader benchmarks should improve generalization. In robotics, however, a model can generalize while work still remains before it can run on a robot with a particular body. The work required differs across methods and target robots, and those differences affect practical deployment. We call the gap between reusable models, representations, or data and their use in execution on the target robot the embodiment gap. This survey examines what can be reused across robot embodiments and what must still be implemented on a new robot. We place existing methods on a two-axis map that shows the type of shared structure and the stage at which adaptation is needed for execution on the target robot. We then examine recent work through three overlapping research directions: sharing semantics and perception, sharing robot data and interfaces, and learning correspondence across embodiments. We also propose a reporting framework for adaptation work that success rate alone does not reveal. The framework identifies the work that should be checked when comparing cross-embodiment learning and highlights work that remains on a new robot and questions for future study.

</details>


### [268] [HarvestPoint-ACT: Explicit Target Selection and Harvest-Point Conditioning for Robotic Fruit Harvesting under Occlusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18446)
*Hanying Hu,Weipeng Li,Yikun Huang,Hao Chen,Zhengtao Hu,Changcai Yang,Weiwei Wan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HarvestPoint-ACT，一种端到端模仿学习框架，通过显式的实例分割与关键点预测来解耦"选果"与"采摘点"两个决策，并在遮挡下保持高成功率（88%，重遮挡下75%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端模仿学习虽避免了手工设计的机器人运动，但策略仍需自行决定采摘哪个果实以及在哪里闭合夹爪。遮挡会导致策略在收获过程中丢失所选果实，且仅从像素中难以推断正确的闭合点。

Method: HarvestPoint-ACT包括三部分：(1) 带关键点分支的实例分割前端，为每个可见果实预测掩码和采摘点；(2) 调度器根据遮挡程度和旅行距离对候选果实排序并选择目标，每次尝试后重新检测和重排序以应对冠层变化；(3) 将所选果实编码为八维状态（绝对采摘点、夹爪到采摘点的向量、有效性标志、置信度）输入动作分块Transformer；当果实暂时不可见时保留上一帧采摘点估计并标记为过期，持续丢失则中止尝试。

Result: 在冠层模拟装置上，HarvestPoint-ACT达到88%的成功率，在重度遮挡条件下仍达到75%的成功率。

Conclusion: 通过将采摘决策显式化到感知层，并结合调度器与状态编码机制，HarvestPoint-ACT在遮挡条件下仍能稳定完成果实采摘任务，验证了显式感知决策对端到端模仿学习的重要性。

Abstract: End-to-end imitation learning avoids hand-made robot motion for approaching and grasping, but the policy must still decide which fruit to pick and where to close the gripper. Occlusion can make the policy lose the selected fruit during harvesting, and the correct closing point is difficult to infer from pixels alone. This paper presents HarvestPoint-ACT, which makes both decisions explicit in perception and provides them to the policy. An instance segmentation front end with a keypoint branch predicts a mask and a harvest point for each visible fruit, where the harvest point specifies the location to close the gripper. A scheduler ranks detected candidates by occlusion and travel distance and selects one target. After each attempt, it redetects and reranks the candidates because the canopy may have changed. The selected fruit is encoded for an action chunking transformer as an eight-dimensional state, containing the absolute harvest point, the vector from the gripper to that point, a validity flag, and a confidence score. When the selected fruit is temporarily undetected, the system retains the last harvest point estimate in the robot base frame and marks it as stale, and aborts the attempt if the loss persists. On a canopy mock-up, HarvestPoint-ACT achieves a success rate of 88%, and of 75% under heavy occlusion.

</details>


### [269] [Backward Layout Search for Sequence-Constrained Robotic Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18454)
*Xi Zhang,Jiancong Dai,Hao Chen,Zhengtao Hu,Changcai Yang,Weiwei Wan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Backward Layout Search (BLS)的机器人装配布局规划方法。该方法利用装配步骤的可行性仅依赖于当前及后续装配零件的初始位姿这一关键观察，以逆向装配顺序分配零件初始位姿，通过几何、运动学、抓取和规定运动检查，结合碰撞掩码和候选集过滤，以及束搜索保留有前景的部分布局，最终在前向装配顺序中通过完整运动规划验证布局。在五个装配模型上的实验表明，BLS能生成无碰撞可执行布局，并减少步骤评估和搜索时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人装配布局规划需要确定装配位置和每个零件的初始位姿，同时保证预设装配序列无碰撞执行。该问题的难点在于每次装配步骤后障碍环境会变化，工作空间中未装配的零件可能阻挡机器人运动，使得传统前向搜索方法效率低下。

Method: BLS方法逆向分配零件初始位姿。关键洞察是每个装配步骤的可行性仅取决于当前和后续装配零件的初始位姿。每次扩展执行几何、运动学、抓取和规定运动检查，使用碰撞掩码和候选集过滤来剔除不可行的初始位姿候选。通过束搜索保留有前景的部分布局，最终布局在正向装配顺序中通过完整运动规划验证。

Result: 在五个装配模型上的实验表明，BLS能够生成无碰撞可执行布局，并且与匹配的前向搜索相比，减少了步骤评估次数和搜索时间。

Conclusion: BLS通过利用装配步骤间的前瞻依赖关系，采用逆向搜索策略有效解决了机器人装配布局规划问题，在效率和可行性上都优于前向搜索方法。

Abstract: Robotic assembly layout planning must determine the assembly site and the initial pose of each part while ensuring collision-free execution of a prescribed assembly sequence. This problem is challenging because the obstacle environment changes after each assembly step, and unassembled parts re maining in the workspace may block robot motions. We observe that the feasibility of each assembly step depends only on the initial poses of the current and later-assembled parts. Based on this dependency, we propose Backward Layout Search (BLS), which assigns initial part poses in reverse assembly order. Each expansion performs geometric, kinematic, grasp, and prescribed-motion checks, while collision masks and candidate set filtering remove infeasible initial part pose candidates. Promising partial layouts are retained through beam selection, and complete layouts are validated by full motion planning in forward assembly order. Experiments on five assembly models show that BLS produces collision-free executable layouts and reduces step evaluations and search time compared with a matched forward search.

</details>


### [270] [DevGRU: Depth-guided Visual Navigation using a Collision-aware Recurrent Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18470)
*Kyung Min Han,Eunsom Kim,Young J. Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DevGRU，一种基于深度图像和点目标条件的导航系统，通过动作预测器和碰撞预测器生成碰撞感知的未来轨迹，在复杂室内环境中有效避免碰撞，并在多个基线上显著提升导航性能，且模型参数少、推理速度快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉导航基础模型在复杂室内环境（如结构化布局和狭窄通道）中容易发生碰撞，导航安全性和鲁棒性不足。

Method: 提出DevGRU系统，包含两个核心组件：1）动作预测器（AP），生成碰撞感知的未来轨迹以避开即时障碍物；2）碰撞预测器，与AP协同工作，补偿目标位姿估计中累积的误差，并主动缓解未来偏差。

Result: 在9个不同场景中与ViNT、NoMaD、NavDP三个SOTA方法及4个ViNT/NoMaD变体对比：导航性能大幅超越ViNT和NoMaD；模型参数量少，推理速度最快，相比NavDP在模型规模上小7倍、推理速度快17倍。

Conclusion: DevGRU通过碰撞感知的轨迹预测机制有效提升了复杂室内环境的导航安全性，同时凭借轻量级设计实现了高效的推理性能，验证了其在视觉导航任务中的实用价值。

Abstract: Existing visual navigation models often aim to develop foundation models that can generalize robot navigation across diverse platforms. However, many of these models are prone to collisions when deployed in complex indoor environments, particularly in structured layouts and narrow passages. To address this problem, we propose a depth image- and point-goal-conditioned navigation system, DevGRU. The proposed system employs an action predictor (AP) that generates collision-aware future trajectories, enabling effective avoidance of immediate obstacles. In conjunction with a collision predictor, the AP further compensates for errors accumulated in the goal pose estimation and proactively mitigates future deviations. To evaluate our method, we conducted experiments across nine different scenes and three state-of-the-art approaches - ViNT, NoMaD, and NavDP - as well as four additional variants of ViNT and NoMaD. In terms of navigation performance, DevGRU significantly outperforms ViNT and NoMaD by a large margin. In addition, the proposed model has a relatively small number of trainable parameters, resulting in the fastest inference time among the baselines, particularly outperforming NavDP by 7x in model size and 17x in inference time.

</details>


### [271] [An Experimental Study of Downwash Effects on a Continuum Manipulator Integrated with a Multirotor UAV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18507)
*Anuraj Uthayasooriyan,Krishna Manaswi Digumarti,ernando Vanegas,Felipe Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文实验研究了多旋翼平台上肌腱驱动连续体机械臂（CM）在下洗气流影响下的运动学偏差，提出了一种基于恒定曲率模型引导的高斯过程回归（GPR）残差修正模型，可显著提升末端位姿预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续体臂空中操作系统利用软体机械臂的柔顺性和灵巧性执行受限或危险环境中的任务，但螺旋桨下洗气流会恶化其性能，尤其是在墙壁和地面附近。目前下洗效应对连续体机械臂的影响尚未被表征，缺乏定量分析和补偿模型。

Method: 通过实验方法，在静止空气条件下将连续体机械臂与恒定曲率（CC）模型进行对比；然后在自由空间四种螺旋桨油门水平下，以及在地面和墙壁附近最大油门条件下，量化下洗引起的末端执行器位姿偏差（相对于静止空气实验基准均值）；最后利用实验数据学习一个CC引导的高斯过程回归（GPR）残差模型。

Result: 垂直位置和偏航角表现出最大偏差，且地面效应会放大这些偏差；CC引导的GPR残差模型将正向位姿预测的RMSE显著改善（位置提升89-95%，姿态提升47-79%）。

Conclusion: 所提出的下洗感知建模方法能够有效补偿连续体机械臂空中操作系统中的下洗诱导偏差，为面向补偿的下洗感知连续体臂空中操作建模提供了有力支撑。

Abstract: Continuum arm aerial manipulation systems leverage soft-manipulator compliance and dexterity for tasks in confined or hazardous environments, but propeller downwash can degrade performance, particularly near walls and the ground. This effect remains uncharacterized for continuum manipulators. This letter experimentally studies downwash-induced kinematic deviations of a tendon-driven continuum manipulator integrated with a multirotor platform. Under still-air conditions, the CM is compared with a constant-curvature (CC) model. Downwash- induced end-effector pose deviations are then quantified relative to the mean still-air experimental baseline at four propeller throttle levels in free space, and at maximum throttle near a wall, and near the ground. Vertical position and yaw show the largest deviations and are amplified by ground effect. A CC-guided Gaussian process regression (GPR) residual model is learned from experimental data that improves forward pose prediction RMSE (position by 89-95%, orientation by 47-79%), and support compensation-oriented, downwash-aware modeling of continuum arm aerial manipulation systems.

</details>


### [272] [Real-Time Control-Constrained DDP for Underactuated Balancing of Legged Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18552)
*SeongWon Nam,Hyunyong Lee,Hansol Kang,Jiman Park,Yeongwoo Son,Bumsu Yi,Jaeyoung Oh,Hyouk Ryeol Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于欠驱动腿式机器人的实时控制约束DDP框架（ABC-DDP），通过加速投影梯度法高效处理控制约束，并在多射击框架中引入虚拟约束以增强可行性，实现了统一的MPC框架下静态双腿站立、慢速猫步、直立行走和高速奔跑等多种运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典DDP在处理控制约束方面存在局限性，难以直接应用于欠驱动腿式机器人实时MPC，且从动态不可行初始条件开始优化时稳定性不足。

Method: 提出基于加速投影梯度法（APG）的控制约束DDP（ABC-DDP），无需重复KKT矩阵求逆即可高效计算约束解并识别活动集；引入虚拟约束将控制约束整合到基于可行性的多射击框架中，支持从动态不可行初始化下稳定优化。

Result: 仿真结果表明该方法能够在统一MPC框架下实现静态双腿站立（对外部扰动鲁棒）、慢速猫步、直立行走和高速奔跑等多种动态运动。这是首次通过实时有限时域MPC实现四足机器人静态双腿站立。

Conclusion: ABC-DDP提供了一种高效、鲁棒的实时控制约束优化方案，能够在欠驱动条件下实现四足机器人多样化的动态运动，展示了其在实时MPC中的实用性。

Abstract: This paper presents a real-time control-constrained Differential Dynamic Programming (DDP) framework for underactuated legged robots. To address the limitation of classical DDP in handling control constraints, we propose an Accelerated Projected Gradient (APG)-based control-constrained DDP (ABC-DDP), which efficiently computes constrained solutions and identifies active sets without repeated Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) inversions. A virtual constraint is introduced to integrate control constraints within a feasibility-driven multiple-shooting framework, enabling stable optimization even from dynamically infeasible initializations. The proposed method supports real-time model predictive control (MPC) with short horizons under strong underactuation. Simulation results demonstrate static two-leg standing under external disturbances, along with diverse dynamic motions including slow catwalk, upright walking, and high-speed running within a unified MPC framework. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first demonstration of static two-leg standing of a quadruped robot achieved using real-time finite-horizon MPC.

</details>


### [273] [LabDex: A Hierarchical Benchmark for Dexterous Manipulation in Laboratories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18618)
*Zhipeng Tang,Sihang Chen,Sha Zhang,Peihao Yang,Yan Liu,Wentao Zhao,Xinrui Liu,Rui Huang,Wensheng Du,Yuting Huang,Jiajun Deng,Lidian Wang,Yuan Zhang,Yanyong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了LabDex，一个面向化学实验室灵巧操作的大规模真实世界数据集与基准，首次在统一框架下联合真实世界和仿真平台，通过层级任务分类（原子技能、组合技能、长时程实验）支持机器人策略的系统训练与评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主实验室旨在加速科学发现，需要机器人灵巧操作多种实验器皿和仪器，并执行长时程、状态依赖的实验流程。然而现有基准未能同时覆盖灵巧手操作、真实实验室交互和多阶段实验流程，限制了系统性的训练与评估。

Method: LabDex采用跨平台设计，首次在统一框架下整合真实世界与仿真平台，提供标准化的任务定义、演示数据和评估协议。其核心是一个三层次的层级任务分类体系：原子技能（基础灵巧操作能力）、组合技能（多项原子技能的组合）、以及长时程实验室工作流。该层级设计不仅支持最终任务性能评估，还能分析基础灵巧技能如何组合并影响复杂实验室操作。

Result: 在真实世界和仿真环境中对代表性机器人学习方法进行了跨层级评估。实验结果验证了LabDex任务设计和演示数据的有效性，表明该基准能够支持现有机器人策略在不同层级的实验室灵巧操作任务上进行训练与系统性评估。

Conclusion: LabDex为自主实验室机器人的进一步发展提供了基础，填补了现有基准在灵巧操作、真实实验室交互和多阶段实验流程联合覆盖方面的空白。

Abstract: Autonomous laboratories hold great promise for accelerating scientific discovery. To achieve this vision, robots are supposed to dexterously manipulate diverse labware and instruments and execute long-horizon, state-dependent experimental procedures. Yet existing benchmarks do not jointly capture dexterous hand use, real-world laboratory interactions, and multi-stage experimental procedures, limiting systematic training and evaluation. To bridge this gap, we introduce LabDex, a large-scale real-world dataset and benchmark for dexterous manipulation in chemistry laboratories, organized around a hierarchical task taxonomy spanning atomic skills, compositional tasks, and long-horizon experiments. First, LabDex is cross-platform and, for the first time, unifies real-world and simulation platforms under a common framework, providing standardized task definitions, demonstrations, and evaluation protocols. Second, LabDex is large-scale and systematically organizes chemistry laboratory operations into three interconnected levels: Atomic Skills, which characterize fundamental dexterous manipulation capabilities; Compositional Skills; and Long-Horizon Laboratory Workflows. This hierarchical design not only supports the evaluation of end-task performance, but also enables the analysis of how fundamental dexterous skills compose and influence more complex laboratory operations. We conduct cross-level evaluations of representative robot learning methods in both real-world and simulation environments. The experimental results validate the effectiveness of the LabDex task design and demonstration data, and show that the benchmark supports the training and systematic evaluation of existing robotic policies across laboratory dexterous manipulation tasks at different levels, providing a foundation for further research and development of autonomous laboratory robots.

</details>


### [274] [Evaluation of Monocular SLAM Systems on High-Altitude Nadir UAV Footage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18632)
*Gašper Spagnolo,Matej Dobrevski,Danijel Skočaj*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统性地评测了五种单目SLAM系统在无人机航拍场景（nadir video）下的表现，发现它们高度依赖环境和轨迹尺度，虽然在短途DJI飞行中MASt3R-SLAM表现最佳（平均水平MAE为参考路径长度的0.53%），DROID-SLAM在整体上最优（2.88%），但垂直方向定位差且大范围轨迹严重畸变，表明当前单目SLAM方法不足以支撑纯视觉无人机导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航空正射视频（aerial nadir video）结合了弱几何约束和严重的感知混淆（perceptual aliasing），是单目SLAM的困难场景。此前缺乏系统性的跨环境、跨尺度的单目SLAM基准评测，特别是在无惯性/GNSS辅助条件下的纯视觉表现，因此本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 对五种单目SLAM系统进行评测，覆盖三类测试场景：本地无人机短途飞行（五组DJI序列）、合成城市级图像以及长距离航拍序列（GES和ALTO）。评测中不提供任何惯性（IMU）或GNSS辅助，以隔离纯视觉性能，主要指标为水平方向平均绝对误差（MAE）占参考路径长度的百分比。

Result: 性能随环境和轨迹尺度剧烈变化：MASt3R-SLAM在五组DJI飞行中取得最低水平MAE（0.53%参考路径长度）；DROID-SLAM在整体上平均表现最优（2.88%）；但在长距离GES和ALTO序列上，没有任何系统能持续保持全局轨迹形状。垂直方向定位普遍较差，大范围轨迹即使在具备回环检测能力的条件下仍高度畸变。

Conclusion: 当前单目SLAM方法在纯视觉条件下不足以支撑可靠的无人机航拍导航，尤其在长距离大范围航拍场景中面临根本性挑战，需要结合其他传感器（如惯性、GNSS）或多模态方法才能实现可靠的空中自主导航。

Abstract: Aerial nadir video combines weak geometric constraints with severe perceptual aliasing, making it a difficult regime for monocular SLAM. We benchmark five monocular SLAM systems on local UAV flights, synthetic city-scale imagery, and long-range aerial sequences. To isolate visual performance, we provide no inertial or GNSS aiding. Performance varies strongly with environment and trajectory scale: MASt3R-SLAM achieves the lowest mean horizontal MAE on the five DJI flights (0.53\% of reference path length), whereas no system consistently preserves global trajectory shape on the long GES and ALTO sequences. Overall, DROID-SLAM performs best, averaging 2.88\% of reference path length across completed runs. Vertical position remains poor, and large-area trajectories remain highly distorted despite loop-closure capability. Current monocular SLAM methods are by themselves therefore insufficient for reliable visual-only aerial navigation.

</details>


### [275] [Progressive Experience Fusion for Multi-Task World Model Control in Endovascular Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18647)
*Harry Robertshaw,Maxence Boels,Nikola Fischer,Sebastien Ourselin,Christos Bergeles,Alejandro Granados,Thomas C Booth*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出渐进式经验融合（PEF）方法训练多任务TD-MPC2控制器，用于自主血管内导航机械取栓术。PEF在十种已知训练解剖结构上达到74%的平均成功率，优于SAC（37%）和基础TD-MPC2（65%）；在30个血管系统训练后，在十个未见血管上达到90%成功率；并成功在体外中风患者血管模型上完成迁移，微调后将路径比从63%提升至80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主血管内导航有望将机械取栓术推广至医疗资源匮乏地区，但控制器需要在不同血管解剖结构中导航长距离、多阶段的路径，这对现有控制方法构成挑战。现有的强化学习控制器在跨血管形态泛化和多任务导航方面表现不足，亟需更有效的训练策略。

Method: 提出渐进式经验融合（PEF）方法训练多任务TD-MPC2控制器；设计基于残差动作序列离散度的启发式方法，动态调整MPPI规划视界；并在患者特定仿真中进行微调以提升跨个体适应性。

Result: PEF在10种已知血管的5个子任务上平均成功率74%（SAC为37%，p<0.001；基础TD-MPC2为65%，p=0.053）。在30个血管系统上训练并采用自适应视界规划后，10个未见血管的平均成功率达90%。在未见的体外中风患者血管模型上，微调（4万步，约107分钟临床转运时间）将路径比从63%提升至80%（p<0.001）。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了多血管系统训练与患者特定自适应相结合的概念可行性，为自主血管内导航的临床应用迈出了重要一步，但仍需在临床部署前进行更广泛的验证。

Abstract: Autonomous endovascular navigation could support the delivery of mechanical thrombectomy to underserved areas, but controllers must navigate long, multi-stage paths across varying vascular anatomies. This study investigates Progressive Experience Fusion (PEF) to train a multi-task TD-MPC2 controller. We additionally evaluate a heuristic that changes the Model Predictive Path Integral planning horizon using residual action-sequence dispersion, and fine-tuning in a patient-specific simulation. Across five subtasks in ten known training anatomies with held-out targets, PEF achieved a mean success rate of 74%, compared with 37% for Soft Actor-Critic (p < 0.001) and 65% for base TD-MPC2 (p = 0.053). A PEF controller with adaptive-horizon planning trained on 30 vasculatures achieved a mean success rate of 90% in ten held-out vasculatures. The PEF agent successfully transferred to an unseen in vitro stroke patient vasculature under fluoroscopy, achieving a mean path ratio improvement from 63% to 80% with fine-tuning (p < 0.001), following 40x103 fine-tuning steps (corresponding to approximately 107 min of clinical inter-hospital transfer time). This work represents a proof of concept for multi-vasculature training and patient-specific adaptation, while further validation is required before clinical deployment.

</details>


### [276] [Orienteering Problem with Uncertain Time-Varying Rewards: Framework and Benchmark for Everyday Service Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18672)
*Masafumi Endo,Kohei Honda,Yuu Jinnai,Ryo Yonetani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的定向越野问题变体——不确定时变奖励定向越野问题（OP-UTVR），允许智能体从观测中估计奖励动态并预测未来奖励，从而在随机奖励变化和预测误差下做出知情路由决策。论文提出了三种不同规划时域和在线自适应程度的规划器，推导了其在奖励随机性下的理论界，并引入了一个移动服务机器人基准测试验证方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有定向越野问题（OP）的大多数公式假设奖励是事先已知的，但实际应用中（如配送代理面对不断变化的客户需求）奖励往往具有不确定性和时变性。这一假设与实际场景不符，限制了OP在真实世界中的应用。

Method: 提出了三种规划器，区别在于规划时域长短和在线自适应程度；推导了各规划器在奖励随机性条件下的理论性能界；构建了一个室内移动服务机器人基准测试，机器人在行人环境中导航。

Result: 实验揭示了规划时域与自适应程度之间的权衡关系，并展示了长时域规划结合在线自适应的有效性。

Conclusion: 结合长时域规划与在线自适应能够在不确定时变奖励环境中取得最佳性能，为处理奖励动态不确定性的路由规划问题提供了新的解决思路和理论支撑。

Abstract: We present the orienteering problem with uncertain time-varying rewards (OP-UTVR), a novel variant of the orienteering problem (OP). While most existing OP formulations assume rewards to be known in advance, practical applications involve uncertain and time-varying rewards, as with shifting customer demand for delivery agents. OP-UTVR relaxes this assumption by allowing agents to estimate reward dynamics from observations and forecast future rewards. This enables informed routing decisions despite stochastic reward changes and inevitable prediction errors. We address this problem using three planners that differ in planning horizon and online adaptivity, and derive theoretical bounds on their performance under reward stochasticity. We further introduce a mobile service robot benchmark for OP-UTVR, where a robot navigates among pedestrians in indoor environments. Experiments reveal trade-offs between planning horizon and adaptivity, and demonstrate the effectiveness of long-horizon planning with online adaptation.

</details>


### [277] [SoftVTBench: A Deformation-Aware Visuo-Tactile Dataset and Benchmark for Deformable-Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18701)
*Bowen Jing,Mingxin Wang,Ruiyang Hao,Chenchen Ge,Hanwen Shen,Junjie He,Yang Cui,Yiming Hou,Weitao Zhou,Jiawei Wang,Minglei Li,Dandan Zhang,Ding Zhao,Houde Liu,Xiaofan Li,Si Liu,Ping Luo,Haibao Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SoftVTBench，一个用于物理交互感知的柔性物体操作基准，包含4,000条专家演示和50多个资产，同步多视图RGB、双指触觉RGB和标记运动、本体感觉、语言、夹爪动作及有限元（FEM）状态。基于该数据集建立了闭环基准，定义了变形感知成功率（DSR），要求任务完成同时峰值归一化变形在容差内。实验表明，在所有12个分布内配置中均存在成功回放违反变形容差的情况，且触觉变体的分布外优势明显但分布内收益有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数柔性物体操作基准仅评估任务成功与否，忽略了物理交互质量（如滑移、过度压缩）。主要瓶颈是缺乏将策略可见的接触观测与独立物理真值配对的全任务视触觉数据集，因此无法评估策略与柔性物体交互的物理质量及触觉对交互质量的实际贡献。

Method: 构建SoftVTBench视触觉数据集，包含4,000条专家演示和50多个资产（体积柔性物体及视觉匹配的刚性孪生体），以20Hz同步多视图RGB、双指触觉RGB和标记运动、本体感觉、语言、二元和连续夹爪动作及仅评估者可用的FEM状态。基于固定物体特异性校准定义变形感知成功率（DSR），在Diffusion Policy、π0.5和FastWAM三个策略上评估12个分布内配置和6个分布外配置。

Result: 所有12个分布内配置中均有成功回放违反变形容差，占各配置成功率的0.7%–24%；分布偏移下，视触觉变体在全部6个策略-套件比较中任务成功率更高，在5个中DSR更高，但分布内收益不一。这表明提供触觉本身并不能保证有效的多模态融合。

Conclusion: SoftVTBench为研究柔性物体操作中的物理交互质量提供了共享的视触觉资源，不仅考察策略是否成功，还考察它如何与柔性物体物理交互以及触觉何时能改善这种交互。

Abstract: Physical interaction quality is central to deformable-object manipulation, yet most benchmarks evaluate task success alone. A policy may complete the task while allowing slip or causing excessive compression. A primary bottleneck is the absence of visuo-tactile datasets that pair policy-visible contact observations with independent physical ground truth over complete tasks. We introduce SoftVTBench, a visuo-tactile dataset for physical-interaction-aware deformable-object manipulation. It contains 4,000 expert demonstrations and more than 50 assets, including volumetric deformable objects and visually matched rigid twins. At 20 Hz, each episode synchronizes multi-view RGB, dual-finger tactile RGB and marker motion, proprioception, language, and binary and continuous gripper actions, alongside evaluator-only finite-element (FEM) states. Building upon this dataset, we establish a closed-loop benchmark that uses fixed object-specific calibration to define the Deformation-aware Success Rate (DSR), which counts a rollout as successful only when it completes the task and keeps peak normalized deformation within tolerance. Across Diffusion Policy, $π_{0.5}$, and FastWAM, all 12 in-distribution configurations contain successful rollouts that violate the deformation tolerance, accounting for 0.7--24% of each configuration's successes. Under distribution shift, visuo-tactile variants achieve higher task success in all six policy--suite comparisons and higher DSR in five, whereas their in-distribution benefits are mixed. These results show that making touch available does not by itself ensure effective multimodal fusion. SoftVTBench therefore provides a common visuo-tactile resource for studying not only whether a policy succeeds, but how it physically interacts with deformable objects and when touch improves that interaction.

</details>


### [278] [Dream2Reward: Transition-Alignment Reward Models from Positive Demonstrations for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18787)
*Haoyu Zhang,Zecui Zeng,Bin Wang,Lusong Li,Liang Lin,Long Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Dream2Reward通过从正样本演示中学习语言条件化的"成功潜在转移场"，以预测的成功状态变化与实际观测位移进行比较（方向一致性和幅度一致性），生成密集因果奖励，无需失败标注或进度标签，在策略学习中有效缓解奖励欺骗并提升下游性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习机器人策略需要密集奖励，但传统的基于进度的奖励在行为偏离成功轨迹后可能仍然给出高奖励，导致无法有效区分低质量行为。现有方法依赖失败标注或进度标签，成本高昂且难以泛化。

Method: Dream2Reward从正样本演示中学习一个语言条件化的成功潜在转移场。模型以转移开始前的视觉历史为条件，预测成功执行对应的潜在位移，并通过带符号的方向一致性和对称的幅度一致性来评估观测到的实际位移。该方法不需要失败标注、进度标签或合成负样本，生成密集因果奖励。

Result: 在机制诊断和共享轨迹评估中，Dream2Reward比基于进度的方法具有更强的成功-失败区分能力和对低质量行为的信息性反馈。在在线和离线策略学习中，同一冻结奖励模型减少了奖励欺骗，提升了下游性能，包括真实机器人操作任务。

Conclusion: 将实际运动与预测的成功变化进行比较，是将正样本演示转化为机器人学习密集奖励的有效方式，为奖励设计提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Learning robotic policies requires dense rewards that remain informative when behavior departs from successful demonstrations. Progress-based rewards estimate how far an observation has advanced along a nominal successful trajectory, but may remain high after an incorrect transition. We introduce Dream2Reward, which learns a language-conditioned successful latent transition field from positive demonstrations. Given the visual history up to a transition start, the model predicts the latent displacement associated with successful execution and scores the observed displacement through signed directional and symmetric magnitude agreement. This transition-level comparison penalizes wrong-direction, overshooting, and stagnant motion even when the resulting observation appears to show progress. Dream2Reward requires no failure annotations, progress labels, or synthetic negatives, and produces a dense causal reward. Across mechanism diagnostics and shared-trajectory evaluations, it provides stronger success-failure separation and more informative feedback on low-quality behavior than progress-based alternatives. Across online and offline policy learning, the same frozen reward model reduces reward hacking and supports stronger downstream performance, including in real-robot manipulation. These results show that comparing realized motion with predicted successful change provides an effective way to convert positive demonstrations into dense rewards for robot learning.

</details>


### [279] [Beyond Placement and Articulation: Usage-Driven Code Scenes for Embodied Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18840)
*Zijian Xiao,Zipeng Ye,Jinkun Hao,Xiong Yang,Yuchen Xie,Ran Yi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoomWright是一个以使用为导向（usage-driven）的智能体框架，用于生成完全以代码表示的3D场景，支持具身智能体的交互环境。它将每个锚点视为任务中心，通过使用驱动的对象推理、代码智能体生成触发器-条件-效果的交互规则，以及注释引导的方向推理，生成可执行、可编辑且可仿真的交互式场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于代码的场景生成方法虽然能生成可编辑和可扩展的环境，但主要关注视觉构建和对象级别的运动学（articulation），缺乏对场景功能性使用（functional usage）的建模，无法为具身AI提供真正可交互的环境。

Method: RoomWright包含三个核心设计：(1) 使用驱动的对象推理（usage-driven object reasoning），将每个锚点视为任务中心，识别任务所需对象及其affordances；(2) 代码智能体（code agent）将每个交互编译为触发器-条件-效果（trigger, condition, effect）规则，更新结构化对象状态并捕获对象间的因果依赖；(3) 注释引导的使用驱动方向推理（annotation-informed usage-guided orientation），解决操控物（manipuland）方向在像素空间中难以恢复的歧义问题。

Result: 大量实验验证了方法的有效性，生成的场景可执行、可编辑、可仿真，为具身AI和策略学习提供交互式环境。

Conclusion: RoomWright通过将功能性使用纳入场景生成流程，弥合了场景视觉构建与功能性交互之间的鸿沟，为具身AI和机器人策略学习提供了更实用的仿真环境。

Abstract: Indoor scene synthesis provides essential environments for embodied AI, robotic manipulation, and simulation-based policy learning. Recent code-based scene generation methods produce editable and extensible environments, yet they remain focused on visual construction and object-level articulation, leaving the functional usage of scenes largely unmodeled. To address this problem, we present RoomWright, an agentic usage-driven framework for generating 3D scenes represented entirely as code for embodied interaction. RoomWright performs usage-driven object reasoning, which treats each anchor as a task centre and admits task-required objects and their affordances. A code agent further enables multi-part interaction by compiling each interaction into a trigger, condition, effect rule that updates structured object states, capturing causal dependencies across objects. Moreover, since manipuland orientation is ambiguous and hard to recover from pixels, RoomWright alleviates this via annotation-informed usage-guided orientation. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our method. The resulting scenes are executable, editable, and simulation-ready, providing interactive environments for embodied AI and policy learning.

</details>


### [280] [RoboEdit: Turning Human Manipulation Videos into Scalable Robot Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18948)
*Yaowei Guo,Zeng Tao,Yuxin Jiang,Yunuo Chen,Zhiyang Dou,Yuxiang Ma,Yin Yang,Demetri Terzopoulos,Ying Jiang,Chenfanfu Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboEdit是一个将人类操作视频转换为与动作一致、物理合理的机器人视频的编辑套件，通过自动管线RoboEdit-ADC生成大规模对齐数据RoboEdit-14M（174K对齐视频对/1400万帧，涵盖7种机器人形态），核心编辑引擎RoboEdit-Trans采用跨形态适应模块和3D机器人状态解码器，在真实世界操作任务中取得了最先进的编辑质量并支持下游机器人控制策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人手-物体交互数据的采集成本高昂且具有形态特异性，而大量现成的人类操作视频因形态差异无法直接用于机器人训练，亟需一种能够将人类视频转化为机器人可用训练数据的方法。

Method: 提出RoboEdit套件，包含三个核心组件：(1) RoboEdit-ADC自动管线，从RGB视频中重建并重定向跨形态的3D交互；(2) RoboEdit-14M大规模数据集，包含174K对齐视频对（14M帧）覆盖7种机器人形态；(3) RoboEdit-Trans核心编辑引擎，采用跨形态适应模块保持时间连贯性同时调整外观和运动，并集成3D机器人状态解码器恢复逐帧手部状态以实现结构化运动监督。

Result: 实验表明RoboEdit在编辑质量上达到最先进水平，并在真实世界操作任务中成功支持下游机器人控制策略。

Conclusion: RoboEdit套件解锁了未标注人类视频的巨大潜力，为通用机器人学习提供了可扩展、高保真的视觉和3D运动监督，有望显著缓解机器人训练数据稀缺的瓶颈。

Abstract: Collecting robot hand-object interaction data is costly and embodiment-specific, yet abundant human-object videos remain unusable for robot training. We present RoboEdit, a human-to-robot video editing suite that transforms human manipulation videos into action-consistent, physically plausible robot videos with aligned 3D hand states. To enable scalable supervision, we introduce RoboEdit-ADC, an automatic pipeline that reconstructs and retargets 3D interactions from RGB videos across embodiments. This pipeline generates RoboEdit-14M, a large-scale dataset of 174K aligned video pairs (14M frames) spanning seven robot embodiments, diverse scenes, and interaction types. The core editing engine, RoboEdit-Trans, employs cross-embodiment adaptation modules to preserve temporal coherence while adapting appearance and motion. It further integrates a 3D Robot-State Decoder to recover per-frame hand states for structured motion supervision. Experiments show that RoboEdit achieves state-of-the-art editing quality and supports downstream robot control policies in real-world manipulation tasks. Ultimately, the RoboEdit suite unlocks the vast potential of unlabeled human videos, providing scalable, high-fidelity visual and 3D motion supervision for generalizable robot learning.

</details>


### [281] [Autonomous Agricultural Tractor: Integrated Weed Detection and LiDAR Navigation for Precision Paddy Farming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19004)
*Benjamin Merryman-Smith,Tony Nguyen,Bilal Dogutas,Krish Shah,Anthony Raphael,Sudip Dhakal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AgriNav，一个集成式自主拖拉机系统，通过四个ROS耦合模块（PyTorch版WeedDet稻穗检测、1.68M参数CNN-FPN变体、不对称逻辑门控保护、6状态EKF导航融合）解决水稻田间杂草定点管理中的三大挑战，并创新性地提出四机制LiDAR-相机融合桥，实现零额外硬件成本的区域约束与置信度融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水稻田间杂草定点管理相比传统喷洒可大幅减少除草剂使用，但实际部署受到三个持续挑战的限制：冠层下GNSS退化时稳健的作物行导航、水稻与形态多样杂草的实时视觉区分，以及将水稻误判为杂草的不可逆不对称成本。现有系统尚未能同时解决这三个问题。

Method: AgriNav由四个ROS耦合模块组成：(1) 自研PyTorch重实现的WeedDet用于水稻检测；(2) 并行的轻量级1.68M参数CNN-FPN变体，采用不对称类别加权；(3) 反向逻辑判别模块，通过硬编码置信度门控否决权保护水稻类别；(4) 融合GNSS、IMU和轮式里程计的6状态恒速转弯率扩展卡尔曼滤波器，具备三级中断桥接。核心创新是四机制LiDAR-相机融合桥：利用导航LiDAR进行感兴趣区域约束、世界坐标投影、地面平面滤波和双向置信度融合，零额外硬件成本。

Result: 仿真实验证明：可在20秒GNSS中断期间保持连续位置跟踪，作物行检测置信度全程高于0.9；在水稻、航拍和洪水后图像中水稻检测置信度达0.32至0.95；LiDAR ROI约束将检测推理区域估计缩减30%至50%。

Conclusion: AgriNav通过软硬件协同的系统级设计，在不增加硬件成本的前提下，有效解决了杂草管理自主系统中的导航、判别和错误代价三大核心难题，为水稻田定点杂草管理的实际部署提供了可行的集成方案。

Abstract: Site-specific weed management in paddy farming offers substantial reductions in herbicide use over conventional broadcast spraying, but field deployment has been limited by three persistent challenges: robust crop-row navigation under canopy where GNSS degrades, real-time visual discrimination between rice and morphologically diverse weeds, and the asymmetric cost of misclassifying rice as weed, which is irreversible.
  This paper presents AgriNav, an integrated autonomous tractor system built around four ROS-coupled modules: a custom PyTorch reimplementation of WeedDet for rice detection, a parallel lightweight 1.68M-parameter CNN-FPN variant with asymmetric class weighting, an inverted-logic discrimination module that protects the rice class through a hardcoded confidence-gate veto, and a 6-state constant-velocity-turn-rate Extended Kalman Filter fusing GNSS, IMU, and wheel odometry with three-level outage bridging. Our primary system-level contribution is a four-mechanism LiDAR-camera fusion bridge that uses the navigation LiDAR for region-of-interest constraint, world-coordinate projection, ground-plane filtering, and bidirectional confidence fusion at zero additional hardware cost.
  Simulation experiments demonstrate continuous position tracking through a 20-second GNSS outage, crop row detection confidence above 0.9 throughout operation, and rice-detection confidences from 0.32 to 0.95 across paddy, aerial, and post-flood imagery. The LiDAR ROI constraint reduces detection inference region by an estimated 30 to 50 percent.

</details>


### [282] [LT-Mem: Volatility-Aware Spatio-Temporal Memory for Lifelong Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19059)
*Yumin Lee,Hyoseok Ju,Giseop Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出LT-Mem，一个波动感知的记忆演化框架，用于长期机器人操作中的跨会话物体级感知与推理。它通过多会话SLAM对齐物体观测，结合波动感知策略管理记忆演化，并引入Tri-Memory结构（Live、Delta、Meta）保持当前状态与事件历史。同时提出LT-VQA数据集进行评估，实验显示LT-Mem在各项指标上优于基线且消耗更少的token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期机器人在演化环境中运行需要持久的物体级理解，但现有系统要么覆盖历史以维持最新地图，要么存储语义快照而缺乏一致的跨会话物体身份，导致"时间失忆"（temporal amnesia）——无法回答诸如"绿色椅子在所有会话中出现在哪里"这类历史查询。这限制了机器人对物体长期状态的推理能力。

Method: LT-Mem由三部分组成：(1) 多会话SLAM主干提供跨会话空间对齐的逐实例物体观测；(2) 推理层通过确定性证据评分保持跨会话身份，并利用波动感知策略在覆盖（overwrite）、保持（hold）和多假设（multi-hypothesis）动作之间选择，以匹配每个物体的动态特性；(3) 采用Tri-Memory结构（Live、Delta、Meta）同时保存当前状态和事件历史，支持纵向物体中心推理。此外引入LT-VQA数据集，包含多会话记录、持久身份标注和时间问答对。

Result: 实验表明LT-Mem在所有指标上均优于基线方法，同时消耗的token数量少了一个数量级。消融实验证实性能提升主要来自结构化记忆架构而非LLM能力。

Conclusion: LT-Mem通过波动感知的结构化记忆演化框架有效解决了长期机器人操作中的时间失忆问题，能够以高效方式支持跨会话的物体级历史查询，且证明了记忆架构设计比单纯依赖模型容量更为关键。

Abstract: Long-term robot operation in evolving environments requires object-level understanding that persists across repeated revisits. Existing systems either overwrite history to maintain an up-to-date map or store semantic snapshots without consistent cross-session object identity, resulting in temporal amnesia: the systematic loss of object history that prevents answering queries such as "Where has the green chair been across all sessions?" We propose LT-Mem, a volatility-aware memory evolution framework that unifies spatially aligned instance-level 3D perception with volatility-conditioned temporal reasoning. First, a multi-session SLAM backbone provides spatially aligned per-object observations across sessions. Second, a reasoning layer governs how object memory evolves: deterministic evidence scoring preserves cross-session identity, and a volatility-aware policy selects among overwrite, hold, and multi-hypothesis actions based on each object's dynamics. Third, the resulting Tri-Memory structure (Live, Delta, Meta) preserves both current states and event histories, enabling longitudinal object-centric reasoning. We further introduce LT-VQA, a dataset and evaluation suite comprising multi-session recordings, persistent identity annotations, and temporal QA pairs. Experiments show that LT-Mem consistently outperforms baselines across all metrics while consuming an order of magnitude fewer tokens, and ablations confirm that gains are driven by the structured memory architecture rather than LLM capacity.

</details>


### [283] [DA-WAM: Decision-Aligned Future Latents for Driving World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19085)
*Ruiguo Zhong,Benshan Ma,Xiaolong Chen,Lang Zhang,Mingyue Feng,Yaonong Wang,Pei Liu,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DA-WAM框架，将预测性表示学习、动作条件未来建模和轨迹评分统一到单一决策目标下，使未来建模不仅具有预测性，还能直接指导轨迹选择，在NAVSIM-v1和NAVSIM-v2上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶世界模型存在关键缺陷：未来建模与规划优化被解耦，或在不同轨迹候选之间共享预测状态，从而稀释了本应指导选择的动作特定后果。这使得未来模型仅具有预测性，而非决策信息性（decision-informative），无法充分发挥世界模型在决策中的潜力。

Method: DA-WAM包含三个核心组件：(1) 预测表示学习与规划优化统一：通过在线编码器和稳定的动量目标在整个规划优化过程中维持预测监督，使未来表示与驾驶任务共同演化；(2) 动作条件预测器：为每条轨迹候选生成独立的未来潜在状态；(3) 未来潜在条件因子化评分器：对每条轨迹的预测未来状态进行评估。对于专家匹配轨迹，预测的未来潜在状态受观测到的未来表示监督，同时在规划边界附近引入安全关键硬负样本提供额外监督。

Result: 在NAVSIM-v1和NAVSIM-v2两个基准上取得了最先进的性能，消融实验和诊断分析验证了各关键组件的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过让未来表示与规划任务协同演化，并结合动作条件预测和未来潜在条件评分，DA-WAM有效弥合了预测性未来建模与决策性轨迹选择之间的鸿沟，为自动驾驶中的世界模型应用提供了新的统一框架。

Abstract: Anticipating how scenes evolve under ego actions is fundamental to safe autonomous driving, yet the full potential of world models for decision-making remains unrealized. The critical challenge lies in ensuring that future modeling is not merely predictive, but decision-informative: the predicted future must directly shape which trajectory is selected. Existing approaches decouple future representation learning from planning optimization, or share predicted states across trajectory candidates, thereby diluting the action-specific consequences that ought to guide selection. To bridge this gap, we propose DA-WAM, a framework that unifies predictive representation learning, action-conditioned future modeling, and trajectory scoring under a single decision-making objective. DA-WAM maintains predictive supervision throughout planner optimization via an online encoder and a stable momentum target, allowing future representations to co-evolve with the driving task. An action-conditioned predictor generates a distinct future latent state per trajectory candidate, which is then evaluated by a future-latent-conditioned factorized scorer. For the expert-matched trajectory, the predicted future latent is supervised by the observed future representation, while safety-critical hard negatives provide additional supervision near planning boundaries. Extensive experiments on NAVSIM-v1 and NAVSIM-v2 demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, while ablations and diagnostic analyses validate the key components.

</details>


### [284] [ADEPT: Accelerating Dexterity via Pre-Training and Post-Training using Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19182)
*Jayjun Lee,Jessica Yin,Asif Rana,Nicholas Blauch,Sam Mady,Mohak Bhardwaj,Nima Fazeli,Nathan Ratliff,Karl Van Wyk,Ankur Handa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ADEPT是一个大规模强化学习框架，通过在通用物体重定位任务上预训练灵巧策略，再将其作为先验用于下游策略的后训练，实现了高自由度机器人从原始视觉触觉感知直接学习长期任务，并在模拟到现实转移中取得成功（23自由度Kuka-Allegro和29自由度Flexiv-Sharpa两个平台）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多指机器人学习新灵巧行为通常难以从零开始（探索困难），且每个下游任务都需要重复学习相同的技能集合。作者希望通过预训练-后训练的范式，利用预训练策略作为行为先验，加速并泛化灵巧操作技能的学习，避免为每个新任务从头训练。

Method: （1）在通用物体重定位任务上对灵巧策略进行大规模预训练；（2）通过行为克隆蒸馏、评论家预热和保守的on-policy更新组成的稳定后训练方案，防止预训练策略的零样本重定位能力在下游微调中退化；（3）引入关节空间Geometric Fabric作为RL策略与机器人之间的中介，安全地利用完整运动学灵巧性；（4）将后训练教师策略蒸馏为基于感知的学生策略，实现零样本sim-to-real迁移。

Result: 在23自由度的Kuka-Allegro（两个RGB相机）和29自由度的Flexiv-Sharpa（两个RGB相机加五个基于视觉的触觉传感器）两个平台上实现了零样本模拟到现实迁移，能够从具有挑战性的初始状态以人类级速度解决长期灵巧操作任务。

Conclusion: ADEPT证明了大规模预训练的灵巧策略可以作为通用先验，显著加速下游灵巧任务的学习，并且通过蒸馏与后训练技巧的结合，能够将复杂策略稳定地迁移到真实世界多指机器人上，实现高速、长时域的灵巧操作。

Abstract: We introduce Accelerating Dexterity via Pre-Training (ADEPT), a large-scale reinforcement learning (RL) framework for learning sim-to-real transferable dexterity across high degree-of-freedom (DoF) robot embodiments that can solve long-horizon tasks directly from raw visuo-tactile perception. ADEPT pretrains a dexterous policy on a generic object reposing task, then post-trains downstream policies with this pretrained behavior as a prior. ADEPT enables learning new behaviors that are otherwise difficult to discover from scratch on multi-fingered robots and avoids learning the same set of skills over again for every new downstream task. The pretrained policy zero-shots the reposing phase of downstream tasks, but naïve RL fine-tuning rapidly degrades this capability during transfer. We address this with a stable post-training recipe combining behavior-cloning distillation, critic warm-up, and conservative on-policy updates. To safely exploit the full kinematic dexterity, we introduce a joint-space Geometric Fabric that mediates between the RL policy and the robot. We distill post-trained teachers into perceptive students that zero-shot sim-to-real transfer on two embodiments: a 23 DoF Kuka-Allegro with two RGB cameras, and a 29 DoF Flexiv-Sharpa with two RGB cameras and five vision-based tactile sensors, and can solve long-horizon tasks from challenging initial states with dexterity at human-level speed.

</details>


### [285] [PartialBiGrasp: Inferring Hidden Local Geometry for Bimanual Grasping from Partial Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19188)
*Ayush Kaura,Vignesh Vembar,Md Faizal Karim,Keshab Patra,K Madhava Krishna*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PartialBiGrasp，一种面向部分点云观测的双臂机器人抓取生成框架。该框架通过卷积占据网络隐式学习几何特征，实现局部可抓取性、无碰撞接触区域及物体厚度的推理，生成力闭合的抓取对，并通过基于采样的优化对不完整几何导致的歧义进行修正。在解析力闭合指标、大规模仿真及真实机器人实验中均验证了方法的鲁棒性和物理稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双臂抓取对大型、重型及几何复杂物体至关重要，但这些物体通常仅包含由局部几何属性（如厚度、边缘结构和夹爪间隙）决定的稀疏可抓取区域。现有双臂抓取方法假设可获取物体的完整点云，而现实场景中往往只能获得部分点云观测，因此需要一种能够直接从部分观测中进行双臂抓取生成的框架。

Method: PartialBiGrasp采用卷积占据网络隐式学习物体的几何特征，使模型能够进行局部推理，包括可抓取性判断、无碰撞接触区域识别和物体厚度估计。基于这些理解生成力闭合抓取对后，进一步利用基于采样的优化进行细化，以校正因几何信息不完整引起的抓取歧义。

Result: 通过解析力闭合指标、大规模仿真实验以及真实机器人对噪声部分点云新物体的评估，PartialBiGrasp展现了鲁棒且物理稳定的双臂抓取生成能力。

Conclusion: PartialBiGrasp证明了在仅有部分点云观测的条件下，通过隐式几何特征学习与采样优化相结合，能够有效生成高质量的双臂抓取方案，为真实场景中双臂机器人操纵大型复杂物体提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Dual-arm robotic grasping is essential for manipulating large, heavy, and geometrically complex objects that cannot be reliably handled using a single manipulator. These large objects often contain only sparse graspable regions determined by local geometric properties such as thickness, edge structure, and gripper clearance. Prior bimanual grasping methods assume access to a full point cloud of the object which inherently contains this geometric information, but may not be accessible in real scenarios. This work proposes PartialBiGrasp, a dual-arm grasp generation framework that operates directly on partial point cloud observations. Our model learns geometric features implicitly through convolutional occupancy networks, enabling local reasoning about graspability, collision-free contact regions, and object thickness. We leverage this understanding to generate force-closure compliant grasp pairs, which are further refined using a sampling-based optimization to correct for ambiguity caused by incomplete geometry. We evaluate our approach using analytical force-closure metrics, large-scale simulation experiments, and real-world robot evaluations on noisy partial point clouds of novel objects, demonstrating robust and physically stable dual-arm grasp generation.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.BM'></div>

# q-bio.BM [[Back]](#toc)

### [286] [Multitask Bayesian Neural Networks for Multiparameter Protein Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18604)
*Fabio Herrera-Rocha,David Medina-Ortiz,Desiree Wyrzykala,Tharun Srinivasan Sudha,Mehdi D. Davari*

Main category: q-bio.BM

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了贝叶斯参数化多任务神经网络在蛋白质多属性协同工程中的表现，基于27个多参数蛋白质数据集和2592个模型组合，发现贝叶斯Last Layer模型在准确率、泛化和校准方面最优，且降维技术可显著提升预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同时工程化多个蛋白质属性仍是一项重大挑战。现有的基于机器学习的蛋白质工程流水线往往将各属性单独建模，未能捕捉属性间的依赖关系和权衡取舍，亟需一种能够在数据稀缺且含噪声的实验条件下实现稳健的蛋白质多属性同步工程的方法。

Method: 研究构建了27个多参数蛋白质数据集，系统比较了从低到全贝叶斯参数化的三种算法架构，结合16种序列表示和降维方法，共计评估2592个模型。重点对比了贝叶斯Last Layer模型与其他架构在准确性、泛化性和校准性能上的差异。

Result: 贝叶斯Last Layer模型在整体准确性、泛化能力和校准性能上表现最佳，在70%的基准数据集上排名第一。降维方法使预测性能提升高达42%，校准性能提升高达57%。简单的One-Hot编码在25%的数据集上达到最优性能，尤其在较大数据集上表现突出。

Conclusion: 研究确立了可靠且数据高效的多参数蛋白质工程的实际设计原则，为选择贝叶斯参数化策略、序列表示方法和降维技术提供了系统性指导。

Abstract: Simultaneously engineering multiple protein properties remains a major challenge. Existing machine learning-based pipelines for protein engineering often model properties separately, failing to capture their dependencies and trade-offs. Here, we systematically evaluate how Bayesian parameterization on Multitask Neural Networks can enable robust simultaneous protein engineering under scarce, noisy experimental data. We curated a comprehensive set of 27 multiparameter protein datasets. Then, we compared three algorithm architectures spanning low to full Bayesian parameterization across 16 sequence representations and dimensionality reduction (2,592 models). Bayesian Last Layer models delivered the strongest overall accuracy, generalization, and calibration, ranking as the top-performing model on 70% of benchmark datasets. Dimensionality reduction improved predictive performance by up to 42% and enhanced calibration up to 57% across architectures. Notably, simple One-Hot encoding achieved top performance on 25% of benchmark datasets, particularly with larger datasets. These results establish practical design principles for reliable and data-efficient multiparameter protein engineering.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [287] [AdaRare: Telemetry-Guided Joint Profile Control for Greybox Fuzzing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18187)
*Jingchuan Ma,Tongan Liu,Yanhua Liu,Qiaoyun Huang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AdaRare是AFL++的一个扩展，通过协调五个内部驱动机制（队列、突变、字典、能量和比较求解）作为单一的有界进程内配置，每5000毫秒更新一次，利用窗口遥测数据驱动加权线性评分器和控制器目标，在多个测试目标上优于vanilla AFL++，并在固件案例研究中发现了五个独立的CVE级内存损坏缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灰盒模糊测试器将交互作用的队列、突变、字典、能量和比较求解控制面组合在一起，而先前的自适应系统通常只优化其他决策对象或控制层，缺乏对这些内部驱动机制进行协调式自适应调节的框架。

Method: AdaRare作为AFL++扩展，将五个内部驱动机制作为一个有界进程内配置进行协调，每5000毫秒更新。它使用已完成窗口的、动作诱导的遥测数据来驱动一个arm-local的recency加权线性评分器（借用disjoint LinUCB的代数结构但作为闭路配置排名机制，而非上下文赌博机的动作值估计器），以及一个配置条件化控制器目标。实验包含三个顺序重复试验阶段（Main、Full、Batch A），并对控制器边界延迟进行测量。

Result: 在Main阶段，AdaRare在全部8个目标上中位数边覆盖率高于vanilla AFL++，其中5个达到Holm显著性。在Full阶段，Full AdaRare在所有5个后续目标上中位数边覆盖率高于CmpLog匹配的AFL++，其中4个显著。Batch A中遥测引导选择在15个目标-控制比较中全部优于固定上下文、随机和轮询调度，13个显著。未建立独立的No-A6-vs-Shadow或scarcity-bundle效应。固件案例研究中发现5个不同的内存损坏缺陷并获CVE编号。控制器边界P99中位数<6.5ms，完整边界P99<14.7ms。

Conclusion: AdaRare证明了协调多个模糊测试内部驱动机制的自适应方案是可行且有效的，在边覆盖率上系统性地优于vanilla AFL++，并在实际固件场景中发现了真实的安全漏洞。但某些内部组件的独立效应和组合效应尚未被充分确立，需要进一步研究。

Abstract: Greybox fuzzers combine interacting queue, mutation, dictionary, energy, and comparison-solving control surfaces, while prior adaptive systems typically optimize other decision objects or control layers. We present AdaRare, an AFL++ extension that coordinates five internal actuation mechanisms as one bounded in-process profile updated every 5,000 ms. Completed-window, action-induced telemetry feeds an arm-local recency-weighted linear scorer and a profile-conditioned controller target. The scorer borrows the algebraic structure of disjoint LinUCB, but serves as a closed-loop profile-ranking mechanism rather than a calibrated contextual-bandit action-value estimator or statistical confidence bound.
  Across three sequential repeated-trial phases, Main provides broad integrated-system evidence: AdaRare has higher median edge coverage than vanilla AFL++ on all eight targets, with five Holm-significant comparisons. In the strongest matched result, Full AdaRare has higher median edge coverage than CmpLog-matched AFL++ on all five follow-up targets, with four Holm-significant comparisons. Batch A finds higher medians for telemetry-guided selection than fixed-context, random, and round-robin schedules in all 15 target-control comparisons, with 13 Holm-significant comparisons. The experiments do not establish independent No-A6-versus-Shadow or scarcity-bundle effects; A6 evidence is target-dependent and weakens under batch-wide correction. In an unmatched firmware case study, AdaRare-generated inputs exposed five distinct memory-corruption findings, each reproduced in a separate environment and later assigned a CVE identifier. Controller-boundary compute P99 medians are below 6.5 ms for a five-second window; complete-boundary P99 medians including synchronous logging are below 14.7 ms. These measurements characterize boundary latency, not total system overhead.

</details>


### [288] [Model Card for OpenAI Privacy Filter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18274)
*Charles de Bourcy,Sahra Ghalebikesabi,Avi Schwarzschild,Alex Gorbachev,Mihai Maruseac,Annie Chu,Vol Kyrylov,Tong Mu,Ally Bennett,Andy Nguyen,Casey Meehan,Jessica Gan Lee,Shane Bauer,Harold Nguyen,Rodolpho Eckhardt,Yuqi Liu,Charlie Oxborough,Marco Rougeth,Omar Chedid,Caio Costa,Yash Parikh,Yao Li,Congzheng Song,Om Thakkar,Vinnie Monaco*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: OpenAI Privacy Filter是一个紧凑的双向标签分类模型，用于检测和编辑非结构化文本中的个人身份信息（PII）和机密信息。该模型通过受限维特比解码器生成八个隐私类别的连贯span，支持精确率-召回率的可配置权衡，拥有15亿总参数、每token 5000万激活参数和12.8万token的上下文窗口，旨在作为分层隐私工作流中的数据最小化组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非结构化文本中检测和屏蔽个人身份信息（PII）与机密信息是隐私保护的关键需求。传统方法在检测精度、推理效率和部署灵活性上难以兼顾，需要一个紧凑、高效且可配置的本地部署方案来满足数据最小化的隐私工作流需求。

Method: 模型从自回归预训练checkpoint派生，转换为双向带状注意力（banded-attention）分类器，可在单次前向传播中对输入序列进行标注。采用受限维特比（Viterbi）解码器产生跨八个隐私类别的连贯span，并暴露可配置的操作点以进行精确率-召回率的权衡。共15亿总参数，每token仅5000万激活参数，支持12.8万token的上下文窗口。

Result: 模型具有1.5B总参数、50M每token激活参数和128K token上下文窗口，设计上兼顾了高效本地部署和领域特定微调能力，并能在精确率-召回率之间提供可配置的权衡选择。

Conclusion: Privacy Filter被定位为分层隐私工作流中可配置的数据最小化组件，而非匿化或合规保证工具。其紧凑高效的设计使其适用于本地部署和领域特定的微调场景。

Abstract: OpenAI Privacy Filter is a compact, bidirectional token-classification model for detecting and redacting personally identifiable information (PII) and secrets in unstructured text. The model is derived from an autoregressively pretrained checkpoint and converted into a bidirectional, banded-attention classifier that labels an input sequence in a single forward pass. A constrained Viterbi decoder produces coherent spans across eight privacy categories and exposes configurable operating points for precision-recall tradeoffs. Privacy Filter has 1.5 billion total parameters, 50 million active parameters per token, and a 128,000-token context window. It is designed for efficient local deployment and domain-specific fine-tuning. Privacy Filter is intended as a configurable data-minimization component within layered privacy workflows, not as an anonymization or compliance guarantee.

</details>


### [289] [XNET: Intelligent Dynamic Sampling for High-Speed Network Security Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18349)
*Thomas Papastergiou,Karthika Subramani,Joseph Reilly,Boladji Vinny Adjibi,Pierros-Christos Skafidas,Roberto Perdisci,Manos Antonakakis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: XNET是一个基于Linux XDP技术的动态采样系统，利用商品硬件在满线速率下监控网络流量，通过按安全价值对数据包分类采样，放大高安全价值流量的可见性，实现最高84%的流量削减、100Gbps的可扩展性以及99.6%的IDS检测率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代企业网络中100GbE线速率日益普遍，传统的流量监控和采样方法面临挑战：昂贵的专用硬件成本高，随机采样导致非刻意丢包和低质量流量，可能漏检隐藏在低速率流量中的关键安全事件（如APT/恶意软件C2通信）。需要一种无需昂贵硬件、不依赖过度采样或复杂分布式部署的线速率监控方案。

Method: XNET利用Linux的XDP（eXpress Data Path）技术实现高效数据包处理，在不占用大量内核开销的情况下在线速率下对数据包进行分类，依据其安全价值进行分级，并根据配置的策略进行动态采样——高安全价值流量被放大采样，低价值流量被削减，从而在整体上缩减流量流的同时增强安全关键部分的可见性。系统设计强调灵活性、可扩展性和动态自适应性。

Result: 在大规模真实网络中部署（仅使用商品硬件），XNET实现最高84%的流量削减且无丢包，同时将原本微不足道流量的可见性提升五倍。受控压力测试证明其可扩展到100Gbps。在IDS应用中，XNET的采样策略实现了99.6%的检测率。

Conclusion: XNET证明了一种基于商品硬件和XDP技术的动态采样方案能够在高线速率条件下同时实现流量削减和安全可见性增强，是传统昂贵硬件方案和随机采样方法之外切实可行的替代方案。

Abstract: Growing network speeds, with 100GbE line rates becoming common in modern enterprise networks, pose challenges to operators and security applications, as they struggle to scale their operational efficiency accordingly, without relying on costly hardware, excessive sampling, or complex distributed deployments. Unintentional loss due to stochastic packet sampling often produces low-quality traffic, further risking missed detection of critical security incidents, particularly those hidden in typically low-rate traffic, such as APT/malware command-and-control communications. In this paper, we introduce XNET, a system that monitors traffic at line rate using commodity hardware and applies dynamic sampling to amplify the visibility of high security value traffic. XNET leverages Linux's XDP technology to process packets efficiently, classify them based on their security value, and sample them as per configured policies. The outcome is a reduced packet stream in which the security-relevant portion of the traffic is amplified at the expense of less interesting traffic segments. XNET is a highly flexible, scalable and dynamic system that can be adapted based on a network's needs. We deployed XNET in a large real-world network using only commodity hardware, where our results show that XNET can achieve up to 84% traffic reduction with no packet loss while increasing the visibility of otherwise negligible traffic fivefold. With controlled stress tests, we further demonstrate XNET's scalability up to 100Gbps. Additionally, we show that XNET sampling led to a detection rate of 99.6% in an IDS application.

</details>


### [290] [Task-Conditioned Least-Privilege Learning for Executable Terminal and MCP Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18351)
*Alexander Tu,Michael Tu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究通过后训练（post-training）教会4B参数模型在可执行终端和MCP环境中选择任务条件化的权限，以减少工具型LLM代理的越权错误。提出的框架在每个动作执行前后沿六个风险维度进行审计，结合任务特定的充分权限包络计算超额权限值并优化后训练，在1,500个任务上训练后，安全成功率从64.36%提升至98.48%，越权错误事件从4.56%降至0.79%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统权限门控系统仅用于验证代理环境是不够的，因为工具型LLM代理在执行任务时可能行使用户未授予或任务不需要的权限，导致越权错误（excess-authority errors）。需要补充性的控制层来教导模型选择任务条件化的权限。

Method: 提出一个框架，其中每个动作在执行前和执行后沿六个风险维度（完成度、证据、确切状态、禁止尝试、安全成功）通过确定性验证器进行审计。结合预定义的任务特定充分权限包络（sufficient-authority envelopes），计算轨迹的任务特定超额权限值（excess privilege values），并在后训练中对其进行优化。在Qwen3.5-4B模型上使用1,500个任务进行训练。

Result: 在2,896个评估片段（涵盖500个留出任务）中，安全成功率达98.48%（基策略为64.36%），越权错误事件从4.56%降至0.79%。外部测试显示能力保持和提示导向的改进。400个任务的延续研究表明泛化能力，越权事件减少6.99个百分点，同时保持先前能力。

Conclusion: 通过最小权限感知的后训练进行学习性约束（learned restraint）可作为可执行终端和MCP环境中工具型代理的额外控制层，但它不能替代权限门控和沙箱。

Abstract: Tool-using large language-model agents can complete a task while exercising authority that the user did not grant or the task does not need, causing excess-authority errors. Traditional permission gating systems alone for validating agent environments are insufficient. We study whether post-training can teach a 4B-parameter model to choose task-conditioned authority in executable terminal and Model Context Protocol (MCP) environments to complement those measures. We propose a framework where each action is audited before execution and again from observed effects along six dimensions of risk. This auditing is conducted using deterministic verifiers that score completion, evidence, exact state, prohibited attempts, and safe success. In conjunction with predefined task-specific sufficient-authority envelopes, we determine task-specific excess privilege values for trajectories, which are then optimized for in post-training. We find that after training using this framework on Qwen3.5-4B over 1,500 tasks, the selected seed reaches 98.48% safe success across 2,896 evaluation episodes spanning all 500 held-out tasks, compared with 64.36% for the base policy, and reduces excess-authority error events from 4.56% to 0.79%. Furthermore, external tests show capability retention and prompt-directed improvement. A 400 task continuation study also found evidence of generalization, reducing excess-authority events by 6.99 percentage points while maintaining previous capabilities. We conclude learned restraint through least-privilege aware post-training is therefore useful as an additional control layer for tool-using agents in executable terminal and MCP environments, but it does not replace permission gates and sandboxing.

</details>


### [291] [0xPass: A Secure Protocol for Universal Cross-Chain Accounts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18359)
*Bernardo David,Keon Kim,Krish Chelikavada*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 0xPass是一个模块化协议架构，用于构建通用跨链账户。它将请求编排、交易求解和交易签名分离为可互操作层，通过阈值签名确保无单一节点持有完整签名密钥，并支持受限授权委托、交易策略、账户恢复和分布式密钥管理。论文还提出了从中心化服务逐步过渡到无需许可网络的渐进式部署路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用账户允许用户通过单一界面跨异构区块链生态系统管理资产和执行操作，但这引入了涉及认证、授权、交易签名、密钥托管、恢复和去中心化等方面的安全与信任挑战。现有方案难以在保持用户控制权的同时平衡安全性与去中心化程度。

Method: 0xPass采用模块化协议架构，将请求编排、交易求解和交易签名分离为可互操作层。用户批准的请求绑定到经过认证的身份并在各层间授权；使用阈值签名防止任何单一交易节点持有完整签名密钥；支持约束性授权委托、交易策略、账户恢复、分布式密钥管理以及各独立运营子网络间的可审计通信。同时设计了从集中服务到许可网络再到无需许可网络的分阶段部署路径，包括第三方模块、抵押担保准入和轮换密钥管理委员会。

Result: 该架构提供了一个实用框架，能够在逐步降低中心化信任的同时扩展跨链账户功能，并保持用户对交易授权的控制权。通过模块化分层和渐进式去中心化部署路径，实现了安全性与可扩展性的兼顾。

Conclusion: 0xPass为通用跨链账户提供了一种切实可行的构架方案，通过模块化分离和渐进式去中心化路线，在安全性、用户控制权和信任分布之间取得平衡，为跨链账户功能的扩展提供了实践指南。

Abstract: Universal accounts allow users to manage assets and execute operations across heterogeneous blockchain ecosystems through a single interface, but they introduce security and trust challenges involving authentication, authorization, transaction signing, key custody, recovery, and decentralization. This paper presents 0xPass, a modular protocol architecture for universal cross-chain accounts. 0xPass separates request orchestration, transaction solving, and transaction signing into interoperable layers. User-approved requests are bound to authenticated identities and authorized across layers, while threshold signatures prevent any single transaction node from holding a complete signing key. The design also supports constrained authorization delegation, transaction policies, account recovery, distributed key management, and auditable communication among independently operated sub-networks. We describe a staged deployment path from a centrally operated service to a permissioned network and ultimately to a permissionless network with third-party modules, collateral-backed onboarding, and rotating key-management committees. The resulting architecture provides a practical framework for extending cross-chain account functionality while progressively reducing centralized trust and preserving user control over transaction authorization.

</details>


### [292] [AoNT Trap: Borromean-Entangled Mutable Chameleon Trapdoor Hash All-or-Nothing Stream Cipher](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18403)
*Victor Kebande*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出BEC-Trap，一种将Borromean互依赖结构、变色龙陷门哈希与流密码相结合的加密方案。密钥(K)、初始化向量(V)和内部状态(St)以Borromean环的方式相互关联，破坏任一组件都会导致整个密钥流崩溃，从而实现计算性的全或无加密（AoNT）特性。变色龙陷门哈希允许受控碰撞，支持无缝的密钥轮换、向量刷新和状态迁移而无需重同步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流密码在密钥管理、状态同步和重密钥操作方面存在效率与安全性瓶颈。作者旨在设计一种既具备强互依赖安全性（任何单一组件的攻破都导致整体失效），又支持高效动态密钥管理的统一加密框架，以满足高吞吐量安全通信、IoT设备和隐私保护区块链通道的需求。

Method: 将密钥(K)、初始化向量(V)与内部状态(St)通过Borromean环结构绑定，使得三者互为支撑、缺一不可；集成变色龙陷门哈希实现受控碰撞，允许在不重同步端点的情况下完成密钥更新、向量刷新和状态轮换；构建计算性AoNT模型，在标准密码学假设下证明互依赖安全性。

Result: 安全分析表明BEC-Trap对密钥恢复攻击、状态泄露和失同步攻击具有抵抗力；在提供机密性、前向保密性和自适应密钥管理能力的同时，保持了较低的计算开销，适用于高吞吐量场景。

Conclusion: BEC-Trap是一种兼顾强安全性（AoNT互依赖）与灵活密钥管理（变色龙陷门）的流密码构造，作为下一代安全通信的基础密码原语具有显著潜力。

Abstract: This work introduces the Borromean-Entangled Chameleon Trapdoor Hash All-or-Nothing (AoNT) Stream Cipher (BEC-Trap), a novel construction that merges Borromean interdependence, trapdoor-enabled mutability, and streaming encryption into a unified framework. The (BEC-Trap) cipher links key (K), initialization vector (V ), and internal state (St) in a Borromean structure, ensuring that breaking, guessing, or removing any one component collapses the entire keystream, providing a computational (AoNT) interdependence under standard cryptographic assumptions. A chameleon trapdoor hash is integrated to permit controlled collisions, enabling seamless rekeying, (V ) refresh, and state rotation without resynchronizing endpoints. This design provides confidentiality, forward secrecy, and adaptive key management with low computational overhead, making it suitable for high-throughput secure messaging, IoT communications, and privacy-preserving blockchain channels. Security analysis of the (BEC-Trap) shows that the construction is resistant to key-recovery attacks, state compromise, and desynchronization attempts, delivering a robust cryptographic primitive for next-generation secure communications.

</details>


### [293] [Beyond Distortion Robustness: Rethinking Severe Cropping as Erasure-Resilient Message Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18567)
*Bo Pang,Weibin Kong,Juntu Dong,Minghan Li,Zhongping Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了CREST，一个从擦除鲁棒性角度重新审视严重裁剪下鲁棒消息嵌入的框架。它将紧凑QR消息通过LT喷泉编码扩展为冗余空间载荷，并结合裁剪感知的嵌入与片段恢复，在COCO、DIV2K和VOC2012上显著提升了严重裁剪场景下的消息恢复率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鲁棒消息嵌入方法大多基于失真鲁棒范式，假设嵌入信号仍空间存在但被噪声、模糊或压缩退化。而严重裁剪会移除载体本身的一部分，导致部分载荷完全丢失而非仅信号损坏，这是一个根本不同的挑战。作者认为严重裁剪应被理解为擦除问题而非传统失真问题。

Method: CREST框架结合了编码理论冗余与神经嵌入/恢复。具体而言，(1) 使用LT喷泉编码将紧凑的QR消息扩展为冗余空间载荷；(2) 采用裁剪感知的嵌入策略将冗余载荷嵌入图像；(3) 通过片段恢复机制从保留区域重建完整消息。整个流程联合设计神经嵌入与编码恢复。

Result: 在混合失真下（面积保留率0.7），CREST将TRA从18.52%提升至68.45%，并将EMR从13.88%降低至4.21%，优于最强基线。在COCO2017上仅保留30-50%图像面积时，CREST仍能达到48.55-65.12%的TRA，而所有对比基线均无法恢复消息。

Conclusion: 严重裁剪更适合被建模为擦除问题而非传统失真问题，将神经嵌入与编码恢复联合设计能有效解决该挑战。CREST作为概念验证框架，展示了这一方向的可行性和巨大潜力。

Abstract: Robust message embedding in images is important for multimedia security applications such as copyright protection and content tracing. Existing methods are largely developed under a distortion robustness paradigm, where the embedded signal remains spatially present but is degraded by noise, blur, or compression. Severe cropping poses a fundamentally different challenge because it removes part of the carrier itself, causing partial payload disappearance rather than mere signal corruption. In this paper, we revisit robust message embedding from an erasure-resilience perspective and present CREST, a proof-of-concept framework for severe-cropping-robust embedding. CREST combines coding-theoretic redundancy with neural embedding and recovery by expanding a compact QR message into a redundant spatial payload via LT fountain coding and coupling it with cropping-aware embedding and fragment recovery. Experiments on COCO, DIV2K, and VOC2012 show that CREST improves recovery under severe cropping while maintaining competitive visual quality. Under mixed distortions with an area retention ratio of 0.7, CREST improves TRA from 18.52% to 68.45% and reduces EMR from 13.88% to 4.21% over the strongest baseline. On COCO2017, CREST still achieves 48.55--65.12% TRA when only 30--50% of the image area is retained, whereas all compared baselines fail to recover the message. These results suggest that severe cropping is better understood as an erasure problem rather than a conventional distortion problem, motivating the joint design of neural embedding and coding-based recovery.

</details>


### [294] [VQC-ZTI: Variational Quantum Control for Zero Trust Protection of the Tactile Internet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18572)
*Mubassir Serneabat Sudipto,Shakil Ahmed,Ashfaq Khokhar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出VQC-ZTI，一种面向触觉互联网服务的分离平面变分量子分类器零信任保护框架。它通过路径外的VQC分析加密流遥测，同时由路径内的策略引擎应用缓存的决定性动作（授权、限制、升级、拒绝），将异常评分与执行分离，从而保持可预测的控制行为并允许独立调优检测敏感度和策略激进程度。在CESNET数据集上，全混合量子神经网络在三种划分方式下分别达到0.9981、0.9974、0.9941的平均AUC，并将误报率相对ExtraTrees降低44.6%、49.6%、67.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉互联网将网络事件与物理执行直接关联，安全决策必须在不扰动控制路径的前提下改善风险判别能力。传统方法难以在不影响实时控制的同时进行有效的异常检测，因此需要一种能够将安全分析与控制执行解耦的框架。

Method: VQC-ZTI采用分离平面架构：路径外（off-path）的变分量子分类器（VQC）分析加密流遥测进行异常评分，路径内（on-path）的策略引擎依据预先缓存的确定性动作（授权、限制、逐步升级、拒绝）执行。通过将异常评分与策略执行解耦，框架允许检测器灵敏度和策略激进程度独立调优。实现上使用PyTorch-PennyLane混合框架，并在CESNET聚合流量上使用随机、实体分组和时间三种划分进行评估。

Result: 在CESNET数据集上，全混合量子神经网络在随机、实体分组和时间三种holdout划分下平均AUC分别为0.9981、0.9974和0.9941，相比ExtraTrees将误报率分别降低44.6%、49.6%和67.9%。组件时序分解显示批量VQC评分处于异步证据路径而非即时执行路径中。

Conclusion: VQC-ZTI通过分离平面的量子分类框架有效实现了触觉互联网服务的零信任保护，在不扰动控制路径的前提下显著提升了风险判别能力。量子神经网络在异常检测精度上优于传统机器学习基线（ExtraTrees），且其异步评分机制保证了控制路径的稳定性和可预测性。

Abstract: Tactile Internet services couple cyber events directly to physical actuation, so security decisions must improve risk discrimination without perturbing the control path. This paper presents VQC-ZTI, a split-plane Variational Quantum Classifier framework for zero-trust protection of Tactile Internet services, in which an off-path VQC analyzes encrypted-flow telemetry while an on-path policy engine applies cached deterministic grant, restrict, step-up, and deny actions. By decoupling anomaly scoring from enforcement, VQC-ZTI preserves predictable control behavior and allows detector sensitivity and policy aggressiveness to be tuned independently. We evaluate the framework on CESNET-derived aggregated traffic using random, entity-group, and temporal holdouts with a hybrid PyTorch-PennyLane implementation. The full-hybrid Quantum Neural Network achieves mean areas under the receiver operating characteristic curve of 0.9981, 0.9974, and 0.9941 and reduces the false-positive rate relative to ExtraTrees by 44.6%, 49.6%, and 67.9%, respectively. A representative component-timing decomposition further illustrates that batched VQC scoring remains in the asynchronous evidence path rather than the immediate enforcement path.

</details>


### [295] [Finality Before Disclosure for Ledger Authenticators in the Quantum Random Oracle Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18605)
*Maja Lie,Benjamin Marsh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文引入 ledger authenticators 概念及 LAEUF 不可伪造性实验，用于分析基于账本的响应式授权协议。识别两个条件性资源边界，并给出联合账本与量子随机预言机执行模型下的安全界 3β_cut²+3c_coKQ²/2^λ+6ℓ/2^λ，最后通过 commit-close-reveal 认证器实例化框架获得多用户终身 QROM 界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共账本（如区块链）越来越多地授权状态转移时不再仅依赖公钥、消息和可移植签名，而是使用先前交易、最终化状态、时间和排序。现有认证模型无法捕捉这种基于账本的响应式授权特性，需要新的形式化安全模型来刻画最终化转录、规范转移新鲜性、自适应腐化、包含前暴露、审查和对抗性排序等威胁。

Method: (1) 引入 ledger authenticators 和 LAEUF 不可伪造性实验，将认证安全性与账本活性分离；(2) 识别两个条件性资源边界——满足单事件条件的认证器产生上下文一次性签名，rebindable reveal 类中的安全性需要计算性后披露非可容许性；(3) 定义联合账本与量子随机预言机执行模型，量子状态在经典最终化切割和账本间 oracle 评估中持续存在；(4) 通过 commit-close-reveal 认证器实例化框架。

Result: 对封闭最终化目标集（大小至多 K），证明安全界为 3β_cut²+3c_coKQ²/2^λ+6ℓ/2^λ，其中 β_cut 衡量切割时已存在的新鲜开具数量。commit-close-reveal 认证器实例化该框架并获得多用户终身 QROM 界。

Conclusion: 该工作为基于账本的授权机制提供了系统性的形式化安全框架，揭示了认证安全性所需的计算性资源条件，并在量子模型下给出了可证明的安全界。

Abstract: Public ledgers increasingly authorize state transitions using prior transactions, finalized state, timing, and ordering rather than only a public key, message, and portable signature.
