We introduce ledger authenticators and $\LAEUF$, an unforgeability experiment for reactive authorization protocols whose public judgment algorithm reads a finalized transcript. The model separates authentication safety from ledger liveness and captures canonical transition freshness, adaptive corruption, exposure before inclusion, censorship, and adversarial ordering. We identify two conditional resource boundaries. An authenticator satisfying our single event conditions yields a contextual one-time signature. Within our rebindable reveal class, safety requires computational post-disclosure non-admissibility. When precursor admission uses only public computation and ledger scheduling, this condition is enforced by closing the evidence eligible to use a disclosed credential. If newly constructed evidence remains admissible after disclosure, censoring the honest reveal gives a forgery. We then define a joint ledger and quantum random oracle execution model in which quantum state persists across classical finalization cuts and oracle evaluations made through the ledger are charged. For a closed finalized target set of size at most $K$, we prove the bound $3β_{\mathsf{cut}}^2+3c_{\mathsf{co}}KQ^2/2^λ+6\ell/2^λ$, where $β_{\mathsf{cut}}$ accounts for fresh openings already present at the cut. A commit, close, reveal authenticator instantiates the framework and obtains a multi-user lifetime QROM bound.

</details>


### [296] [IriSig-Spoof: A Real-World Benchmark for Time-Robust Satellite RF Fingerprinting and Spoofing Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18642)
*Shichang Guo,Yuanyu Zhang,Shuangrui Zhao,Ji He,Pinchang Zhang,Yulong Shen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了IriSig-Spoof，一个基于真实Iridium卫星信号的大规模射频指纹（RFF）数据集与基准，包含517万条消息、66颗卫星、32天的数据以及SDR生成的内外场景欺骗信号，并建立了时间鲁棒性、开放集识别和跨场景欺骗检测三个基准任务，实验表明MACNN模型在最佳配置下达到97.75%的跨日准确率和0.9715的AUROC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LEO卫星互联网成为关键通信基础设施，但其开放无线链路易受卫星仿冒和信号欺骗攻击。射频指纹（RFF）利用发射机硬件缺陷可作为防御手段，然而现有卫星RFF方法的可靠性难以评估——缺乏统一的、支持时间维度、开放集和跨场景评估的数据集与基准。

Method: 构建了IriSig-Spoof数据集：从66颗Iridium卫星收集32天的517万条真实消息，并使用软件定义无线电（SDR）在室内外场景生成欺骗信号。在此基础上建立三个基准任务：(1)时间鲁棒性评估；(2)开放集RFF识别（含未知信号拒绝）；(3)跨场景欺骗检测。采用多尺度注意力卷积神经网络（MACNN）作为基准模型。

Result: MACNN在最佳配置下实现97.75%的平均跨日准确率；开放集评估中AUROC达0.9715，但发现有效的未知信号拒绝并不必然保证可靠的身份分配；跨场景实验在低误报率下表现出显著差异。

Conclusion: IriSig-Spoof为评估时间变化和攻击条件变化下鲁棒的RFF方法提供了可复现的基准，揭示了卫星RFF在开放集场景和跨场景条件下的挑战与局限性。

Abstract: Low Earth orbit (LEO) satellite Internet is becoming critical communications infrastructure, yet its open wireless links remain vulnerable to satellite impersonation and signal spoofing. Radio frequency fingerprinting (RFF) offers a potential defense by exploiting transmitter-specific hardware imperfections manifested in received signals. However, the reliability of existing satellite RFF methods remains difficult to assess because no unified dataset and benchmark support temporal, open-set, and cross-scenario evaluation. To address this gap, we introduce IriSig-Spoof, a real-world Iridium dataset comprising 5.17 million messages collected from 66 satellites over 32 days, together with software-defined radio (SDR)-generated spoofing signals from indoor and outdoor settings. We further establish three benchmark tasks: temporal robustness evaluation, open-set RFF identification with unknown-signal rejection, and cross-scenario spoofing detection. Experiments using a multi-scale attention convolutional neural network (MACNN) show that temporal robustness varies across configurations, with the best configuration achieving 97.75% average cross-day accuracy. In open-set evaluation, MACNN achieves an area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUROC) of 0.9715, while showing that effective unknown-signal rejection does not necessarily ensure reliable identity assignment. Cross-scenario experiments reveal differences at low false-positive rates. IriSig-Spoof provides a reproducible basis for evaluating robust RFF methods under temporal variation and changing attack conditions.

</details>


### [297] [Improving LLM-Based SSH Honeypots Through Prompting and Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18686)
*Muris Sladić,Veronica Valeros,Eman Alibalić,Sebastian Garcia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究如何通过提示设计和监督微调来提升本地开源权重LLM驱动的SSH蜜罐的shell仿真准确性，并使用34个自动化单元测试评估了8个模型（包括原始GPT-3.5及7个本地模型），发现提示设计效果显著，微调效果依赖数据集覆盖度，但两者的优势并不能简单叠加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的SSH蜜罐通常依赖闭源云模型以获得良好的shell真实感，但云模型存在版本不稳定、供应商端变更、攻击者驱动成本和模型停用等部署问题。本地开源权重模型虽避免了这些问题，但性能较差且会出现暴露蜜罐的错误。因此需要研究如何提升本地模型的shell仿真准确性。

Method: 使用提示设计（prompt design）和监督微调（supervised fine-tuning）两种方法。总共微调并评估了8个模型：原始shelLM中使用的GPT-3.5模型和7个开源权重本地模型（每个与基础模型对比）。还测试了提示结构在模型族间的迁移性，并使用34个自动化单元测试在单会话和新会话两种设置下衡量shell仿真准确性。微调数据集包含原始的112段对话数据集和从蜜罐日志构建的扩展数据集。

Result: 提示设计对shell仿真准确性有显著影响；微调效果取决于数据集覆盖度。在原始112段对话数据集上微调未能提升整体通过率，而基于蜜罐日志构建的扩展数据集能显著增强本地模型性能。

Conclusion: 提示设计和微调各自都能独立提升本地LLM蜜罐的效果，但两者效果并不能直接叠加——强基于规则的提示和基于监督的适配可能因处理重叠的shell行为约束而产生冲突。

Abstract: LLM-based SSH honeypots often use closed cloud LLMs because they give strong shell realism, but cloud models create deployment problems. These include no stable versioning, provider-side changes, attacker-driven cost, and model decommissioning. Local open-weight models avoid these problems, but they usually perform worse and make mistakes that reveal the honeypot. These mistakes include malformed outputs, command echoing, inconsistent filesystem state, and AI-style artifacts. This paper studies how to improve and evaluate the shell emulation accuracy of local LLM-based SSH honeypots using prompt design and supervised fine-tuning. We fine-tune and evaluate eight models in total: the original fine-tuned GPT-3.5 model used in shelLM and seven open-weight local models, each compared to its base model. We also test how prompt structure transfers across model families. Using 34 automated unit tests that measure shell emulation accuracy in single-session and fresh-session settings, we find that prompt design has a large effect and that fine-tuning depends on dataset coverage. Fine-tuning on the original 112-conversation dataset does not improve aggregate pass rate, while an expanded dataset built from honeypot logs produces clearly stronger local models. Taken together, the results suggest that prompting and fine-tuning can each improve local LLM honeypots on their own, but their effects do not combine straightforwardly, since strong rule-based prompting and supervised adaptation can also conflict by addressing overlapping shell-behavior constraints.

</details>


### [298] [Autonomous Cyber Defense in Connected Vehicles: A Multi-Agent Approach to V2X Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19135)
*Krishna Teja Medam*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个三层多智能体架构，用于在100毫秒的V2X安全消息处理窗口内检测伪造的Basic Safety Message。车辆端代理在10ms内将消息分类为接受/丢弃/隔离/升级四种动作，边缘代理在50ms内融合多车辆威胁评估并解决安全-安全冲突，云端通过拜占庭容错联邦学习持续优化检测模型。所有时间预算均严格源自SAE J2735和ETSI EN 302 637-2标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有入侵检测系统按单车、单消息、静态规则运行，无法应对联网车辆中安全(Safety)与安全(Security)耦合的挑战——即区分伪造的紧急制动警报与真实的紧急警报。同时，攻击模式可能跨车队、跨时间浮现，现有系统对此视而不见。作者将100毫秒时序约束视为硬性设计需求而非性能目标，提出了一个全新的处理框架。

Method: 提出三层多智能体架构：(1) 车辆端代理在10ms内将每条V2X消息分为Accept、Drop、Quarantine、Escalate四类动作，不确定时偏向Escalate以避免丢弃合法警报；(2) 路边边缘代理在50ms内融合多辆车的威胁评估，利用互补传感器观测解决安全-安全冲突；(3) 云端代理通过拜占庭容错联邦学习精炼检测模型并分发更新权重。所有时间预算直接推导自SAE J2735和ETSI EN 302 637-2规定的100ms基本安全消息周期。

Result: 作者论证了现有框架无法同时为三个部署层级分配基于标准的延迟预算，也未将安全-安全冲突解决作为一等设计约束。本文填补了这一空白，但未给出实验验证结果，属于架构设计提案。

Conclusion: 该三层架构将时序约束作为核心设计原则，在不确定时优先升级而非丢弃，巧妙化解了安全与安全之间的张力。然而，边缘节点的对抗性投毒和自主安全响应的监管框架缺失仍是待解难题，论文将其列为未来工作。

Abstract: A connected vehicle has roughly 100 milliseconds to decide whether an incoming Basic Safety Message is real or fabricated. If a false emergency braking alert reaches the planning pipeline in time, the car brakes - a safety failure triggered by a security failure. Existing intrusion detection systems are not designed to handle that coupling. They operate per vehicle, per message, with static rules - blind to attack patterns that only emerge across a fleet or over time, and blind to the fundamental tension between dropping a suspicious message and dropping a real emergency alert. We propose a three-tier multi-agent architecture that treats this timing constraint as a hard design requirement, not a performance target. At the vehicle level, an onboard agent classifies each incoming V2X message into one of four actions - Accept, Drop, Quarantine, or Escalate - within a 10-millisecond budget, deliberately biased toward Escalate when uncertain, passing ambiguous cases to the roadside edge agent rather than risking a dropped legitimate alert. The edge agent operates across a roadside unit zone with a 50-millisecond budget, fusing threat assessments from multiple vehicles and resolving safety-security conflicts using complementary sensor observations. The cloud tier refines detection models through Byzantine fault-tolerant federated learning and redistributes updated weights to the fleet. Every timing constraint derives directly from the 100-millisecond Basic Safety Message cycles mandated by SAE J2735 and ETSI EN 302 637-2. No existing framework simultaneously assigns standards-grounded latency budgets to all three deployment tiers while treating safety-security conflict resolution as a first-class design constraint. Remaining open problems - adversarial poisoning at the edge and the absence of regulatory frameworks for autonomous security response - are discussed as future work.

</details>


### [299] [CauSec: Unboxing the Causal Drivers of Static Vulnerability Analysis Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18876)
*Md Akram Khan,Daniel Rodriguez-Cardenas,Alejandro Velasco Dimate,Denys Poshyvanyk,Adwait Nadkarni*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出CAUSEC，一个因果分析框架，旨在测试静态应用安全测试（SAST）工具中隐含假设的有效性。通过系统性文献综述识别出57个安全假设，并将假设抽象形式化，结合因果建模与效应估计来验证假设是否成立及其影响性能的因素。实验在四个主流工具上用57,038个告警的人工标注数据集验证了一个常见假设，提炼出3条对未来工作的启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAST工具常通过牺牲检测能力来换取更高性能（如精度、运行时、可扩展性），这些设计选择依赖于对目标代码或分析技术的假设。关键问题是：这些牺牲是否真的带来了预期性能收益？即底层假设是否成立？现有研究缺乏对此的系统性验证方法。

Method: CAUSEC框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 将SAST工具的假设形式化为"安全假设"（security assumption）抽象；(2) 结合假设驱动的因果建模与效应估计（assumption-driven causal modeling with effect estimation）；(3) 验证假设的有效性并调查影响假设的因素。此外，通过对检测crypto-API误用的SAST工具进行系统性文献综述，发现并定性分析了57个假设。

Result: 在四个高度相关的工具上测试了一个流行假设，使用包含57,038个告警的人工标记基准数据集。分析产生了关于假设和因果效应的多项关键发现，并提炼为3条对未来工作的启示（takeaways）。

Conclusion: SAST工具的假设本质上是因果性的，CAUSEC框架能够使这些假设变得可测试，并解释性能变化背后的因果机制而不仅仅是相关性。检验假设的有效性对理解SAST工具设计选择的合理性至关重要。

Abstract: Static Application Security Testing (SAST) tools are widely used in both industry and academia. Such tools often make design choices that sacrifice detection to achieve higher performance, i.e., increased precision, decreased runtime, or increased scalability. These design choices rely on certain assumptions regarding the target code or the analysis technique itself. Hence, the assumptions directly impact the detection outcome through the design choices they influence. This motivates a key question: do the sacrifices in the detection capabilities actually help tools achieve the expected performance gains? That is, are the underlying assumptions valid?
  This paper seeks to address this question by relying on a key observation that the assumptions made by these tools are generally of a causal nature. We propose CAUSEC, a causal analysis framework that makes SAST assumptions testable and explains why the performance changes given certain assumptions, beyond simple correlations. CAUSEC formalizes the assumptions of the SAST tool into the abstraction of a security assumption and combines assumption-driven causal modeling with effect estimation and validation to test its validity and investigate the factors affecting it. To understand what security assumptions generally entail, we perform a systematic literature review of SASTs that detect crypto-API misuse, leading to the discovery and qualitative analysis of 57 assumptions. We then demonstrate the utility and robustness of CAUSEC by testing a popular assumption in four highly relevant tools, using a manually labeled ground truth dataset consisting of 57,038 alerts. Our analysis leads to several key findings that represent insights regarding assumptions and causal effects, which we distill into 3 takeaways for future work.

</details>


### [300] [Who Can Make the Action Happen? An Authority-Decomposition Framework for High-Risk Automated Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18965)
*Mengting Wu,Lin Wang,Yong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于行动（action-relative）的方法，用于推导哪些信任域联盟足以引起受保护执行（受保护的受保护状态转换的发生），并区分对执行的控制与对权威记录的控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险的自动化系统将控制分散在服务、凭据、受保护组件和生命周期机制中。诸如"已授权"、"已批准"、"特权"或"受保护"这类标签无法回答一个基本的因果问题：哪些行为者实际上能够使一个关键行动发生？需要一个基于行动的方法来推导足以导致受保护的执行发生的信任域联盟。

Method: 框架对组件、权力、资源、边界和替代实现结构进行建模；涵盖更新、恢复、覆盖、禁用和替代调用；将执行性因果控制与对操作的权威记录的控制分离开来；推导包含最小充分联盟，并测试所声称的执行边界是否独立于指定的上游域。

Result: 跨领域分析案例说明了该方法。在Havenlon协议模型中，普通见证者需要五个信任域，而证书替换产生三域包含最小已知需求集；Linux域不足以完成完整转换。已部署的全局不可绕过性和边界否决覆盖仍未解决。

Conclusion: 该框架是一个概念性和分析性工具。它不认证实现、不建立部署安全性、不保证完整发现隐藏权力、也不定义证据验证语义。

Abstract: High-risk automated systems distribute control across services, credentials, protected components, and lifecycle mechanisms. Labels such as authorized, approved, privileged, or protected therefore do not answer a basic causal question: which actors can actually make a consequential action occur? This paper provides an action-relative method for deriving which trust-domain coalitions are sufficient to cause protected execution, defined as the occurrence of a designated protected state transition. The framework models components, powers, resources, boundaries, and alternative realization structures; includes update, recovery, override, disablement, and alternative invocation; and separates causal control over execution from control over the authoritative account of an operation. It derives inclusion-minimal sufficient coalitions and tests whether claimed execution boundaries remain independent of designated upstream domains. Cross-domain analytical cases illustrate the method. In a split-control, release-intended, open-state, source-bounded Havenlon protocol model, the ordinary witness requires five trust domains, while certificate replacement yields a three-domain inclusion-minimal known requirement set among source-enumerated protocol witnesses; the Linux domain remains insufficient for the complete transition. Deployed global non-bypassability and boundary-bound veto coverage remain unresolved. The framework is a conceptual and analytical tool. It does not certify implementations, establish deployment security, guarantee complete discovery of hidden powers, or define evidence-verification semantics.

</details>


### [301] [Catastrophic Learning: A New Attack Vector on Continual Learning Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18976)
*Benedikt Kluss,Niklas Bunzel*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次识别并形式化了持续学习（CL）中的一个新安全漏洞——"学习阻断器"（learning blockers），即攻击者通过操纵数据来降低当前或后续迭代的可学习性（攻击塑性），而非传统攻击中仅针对稳定性来重新引发灾难性遗忘。作者提出了六种攻击策略，在MNIST和Split-CIFAR10上针对三种CL方法（DER、ER-ACE、iCaRL）进行了超过4480次模拟实验，证明了攻击者可以同时阻碍新知识获取并促进旧知识遗忘，从而引发"灾难性学习"场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对持续学习的对抗性研究主要集中于重新引发灾难性遗忘（攻击稳定性、降低可用性），但忽略了攻击模型塑性这一维度。作者识别出一个新的安全缺陷：攻击者操纵数据可以降低当前或后续迭代的可学习性，这类攻击在训练当前迭代时难以检测，因为它们可以针对模型尚未遇到的数据。需要对此类攻击进行正式化定义、威胁建模和攻击策略设计。

Method: 作者形式化了"学习阻断器"攻击场景并提出了威胁模型，设计了六种攻击策略：Label-Exchange（标签交换）、Tensor-Exchange（张量交换）、Attraction-Coincident（吸引-同迭代）、Attraction-Preceding（吸引-前迭代）、Repulsion-Coincident（排斥-同迭代）和Repulsion-Preceding（排斥-前迭代）。Attraction变体最小化毒化迭代与受害迭代标签之间的损失，在特征空间中拉近表示；Repulsion变体最大化该损失，推开表示以对抗稳定性机制。Coincident变体中毒化迭代与受害迭代重合，仅使用干净参考迭代作为标签来源；Preceding变体中毒化迭代先于受害迭代，通过扭曲表示使其不可学习。

Result: 在MNIST和Split-CIFAR10数据集上，针对DER、ER-ACE和iCaRL三种持续学习策略进行了超过4480次模拟。结果表明CL系统存在强烈脆弱性：攻击者可以选择性地阻碍塑性以妨碍新知识获取，同时促进旧知识的丢失，从而诱发灾难性学习场景。

Conclusion: 持续学习系统在学习塑性维度上存在显著的安全漏洞。学习阻断器攻击不仅难以被检测（因为可针对未来未见数据），还能同时作用于稳定性和塑性两个维度，导致比传统灾难性遗忘攻击更严重的系统性退化——灾难性学习。这呼吁CL社区需要开发针对塑性攻击的防御机制。

Abstract: Continual Learning (CL) enables deep learning models to iteratively learn from a stream of data without forgetting prior knowledge. Existing adversarial research on CL primarily aims to re-enable catastrophic forgetting, attacking stability and reducing availability. We identify a novel security flaw: data manipulated by an attacker can reduce the learnability of current or upcoming iterations. We term such manipulations learning blockers, as they attack the plasticity of CL algorithms. They are particularly harmful because they are difficult to detect during training of the current iteration, since they can target iterations whose data the model has not yet encountered. When learning blockers additionally induce catastrophic forgetting, the resulting overall degradation is what we call catastrophic learning. We formalize this scenario, define a threat model and propose six attack strategies: Label-Exchange, Tensor-Exchange, Attraction-Coincident, Attraction-Preceding, Repulsion-Coincident, and Repulsion-Preceding. The Attraction variants minimize the loss between the poisoned and the victim iteration label, pulling their representations together in feature space; the Repulsion variants maximize this loss, pushing them apart so stability mechanisms resist the required parameter shift. In the Coincident variants, the poisoned and the victim iteration coincide, using a clean reference iteration only as a label source; in the Preceding variants, the poisoned iteration precedes the victim, leaving it unlearnable due to distorted representations. We evaluate on MNIST and Split-CIFAR10 against three CL strategies - DER, ER-ACE, and iCaRL - across more than 4,480 simulations. Our results demonstrate a strong vulnerability: an adversary can selectively impede plasticity to hinder the acquisition of new knowledge, while promoting loss of prior knowledge, inducing a catastrophic learning scenario.

</details>


### [302] [A 12-Step Process for Industrial Internet of Things (IIoT) Forensics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18991)
*Victor Kebande*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个针对工业物联网（IIoT）环境的十二步数字取证流程（TSP），旨在应对传统取证方法在处理IIoT复杂性、规模性和异构性方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IIoT在关键基础设施（制造业、医疗、交通）中的大规模部署带来了数字取证（DF）的新挑战，而传统DF方法无法有效应对IIoT环境的复杂性、规模性和异构性，亟需定制化的取证流程。

Method: 提出一个专为IIoT事件设计的十二步流程（Twelve-Step Process, TSP），每个步骤从取证准备到调查结束，涵盖潜在数字证据（PDE）的收集、分析和法律合规，旨在提高取证场景中证据的可采性。

Result: 论文首先探讨IIoT在工业生态系统中的重要性，随后检视现有DF挑战，最终通过系统化的十二步流程为IIoT事件提供结构化的调查框架。

Conclusion: 该十二步取证流程为IIoT环境的数字取证提供了全面且定制化的解决方案，能够确保PDE的稳健收集、分析和法律合规，从而提升证据在法律程序中的可采性。

Abstract: The increasing deployment of the Industrial Internet of Things (IIoT) in critical infrastructure sectors like manufacturing, healthcare, and transportation has shown new challenges for Digital Forensics (DF). Traditional DF methodologies are not well equipped to handle the complexity, scale, and heterogeneity of IIoT environments. This paper introduces a comprehensive Twelve-Step Process (TSP) tailored specifically for IIoT incidents, addressing the need for effective investigation and Potential Digital Evidence (PDE) handling in such dynamic environments in DF. We begin by exploring the importance of IIoT and its role in industrial ecosystems, followed by an examination of existing DF challenges. Each step of the process, from forensic readiness to investigation closure, is designed to ensure robust PDE collection, analysis, and legal compliance to increase chances of admissibility from a DF scenario

</details>


### [303] [From Threat Intelligence to Detection: Knowledge-driven Enrichment and Template-based Rule Grounding for Automated Sigma Rule Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19011)
*Sepehr Ghaffarzadegan,Boubakr Nour,Makan Pourzandi,Mourad Debbabi,Chadi Assi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AUTOSIGMA是一个自动化系统，能够将非结构化的网络威胁情报（CTI）报告转换为可执行的Sigma检测规则。它通过结构化的知识库增强输入内容，匹配现有Sigma规则库，并利用LLM-as-a-Judge机制迭代验证规则，在规则有效性、相关性、MITRE ATT&CK技术覆盖率和鲁棒性方面优于现有方法和LLM模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高级持续性威胁（APTs）快速演变，传统手工编写Sigma规则容易出错且需要大量专业知识，限制了可扩展性。虽然已有开源和商业Sigma规则库，但它们跟不上新兴威胁且需要频繁定制以适应不同运营环境，因此迫切需要能够动态生成适应新兴攻击技术和特定用例的Sigma规则。

Method: AUTOSIGMA采用三阶段流水线：(1) 利用结构化知识库对不完整的CTI输入进行信息增强；(2) 将增强后的内容与现有Sigma规则存储库进行模板匹配和规则接地；(3) 采用LLM-as-a-Judge机制对生成的规则进行迭代验证和优化，确保准确性和上下文相关性。

Result: 在多个真实APT报告和多个安全博客上的评估表明，AUTOSIGMA在规则有效性、规则相关性、MITRE ATT&CK技术覆盖率和输入质量鲁棒性方面均优于替代解决方案和纯LLM模型。

Conclusion: 通过结合知识驱动的信息增强、模板引导的规则接地和多阶段验证流程，AUTOSIGMA能够生成准确、上下文感知且高度相关的Sigma检测规则，有效解决了CTI到检测能力动态转化的关键问题。

Abstract: Mechanisms for dynamically converting cyber threat intelligence (CTI) into actionable detection capabilities are necessary due to the rapid evolution of Advanced Persistent Threats (APTs). Sigma rules are an essential part of contemporary threat detection workflows because they offer a platform-independent framework for expressing detection logic that can be converted into particular queries across SIEM systems. Conventional techniques for manually crafting Sigma rules are prone to mistakes, and necessitate extensive knowledge, which restricts their scalability. Although there are open-source and industry-maintained Sigma rule repositories, they often fail to keep pace with emerging threats and require frequent customization to fit diverse operational environments. This emphasizes the necessity of dynamic rule generation that is adapted to evolving attack techniques as well as particular use cases. In this work, we design AUTOSIGMA, an automated solution for transforming unstructured CTI reports into relevant Sigma rules. Rather than relying solely on language models, AUTOSIGMA leverages a structured knowledge base to enrich partial inputs, matches the enriched content against a repository of existing Sigma rules, and then employs an LLM-as-a-Judge mechanism to iteratively validate the rules. By combining knowledge-driven enrichment, template-based rule grounding, and a multi-stage solution, AUTOSIGMA enables accurate, context-aware, and relevant rule generation. Evaluations across multiple real-world APT reports and multiple security blogs demonstrate that AUTOSIGMA outperforms alternative solutions and LLM models in rule validity, rule relevancy, MITRE ATT&CK technique coverage, and robustness to input quality.
  AUTOSIGMA's Demo: https://youtu.be/iSr6IurQ6BM

</details>


### [304] [Malformer: A Multi-Modal Malware Detector Using Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19052)
*Samuel Howard,Kshitiz Aryal,Mahmoud Abdelsalam,Maanak Gupta,Andrew Wheeler,Pradip Kunwar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Malformer是一种四模态恶意软件检测模型，融合Windows可执行文件的文本、图像、图结构和音频四种表示，利用多模态Transformer融合提升检测性能，在201,549个样本上达到98.3%的准确率和0.9833的F1分数，比单模态和双模态基线高出4.6-17.6个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统恶意软件检测系统依赖单一表示（模态）无法有效识别新型威胁，且单一模态提取可能依赖于反汇编的成功率，导致新的失败模式。已有工作仅集成更多模态或更具判别力的表示，但未充分探索多模态融合的潜力。作者旨在通过四模态融合为检测器提供更全面的信息，提升泛化能力和鲁棒性。

Method: Malformer采用了四模态架构：两个RoBERTa编码器分别处理文本和图谱数据（推测），一个修改版Vision Transformer处理图像数据，WavLM处理音频数据；并通过自适应损失加权（adaptive loss-weighting）方案融合各模态特定的表示，从而实现多模态Transformer融合。

Result: 在包含201,549个二进制样本的数据集上，Malformer达到了98.3%的准确率和0.9833的F1分数，优于所有单模态基线和双模态检测器，性能提升幅度为4.6-17.6个百分点。

Conclusion: 多模态融合为抵御日益增长的恶意软件威胁提供了有前景的基础，赋予防御者更通用且更具弹性的检测能力，证明了综合利用多种表示能够显著提升Windows恶意软件检测性能。

Abstract: Traditional malware detection systems that rely on a single representation of malware often fail to identify novel threats. These representations of malware binaries, also known as modalities, do not provide the models with sufficient information to discriminate among all samples. Additionally, individual representations introduce new failure modes, with some modality extraction being dependent upon the success of disassembling. Past works have integrated either additional modalities or more discriminative representations for classification. In this work, we present Malformer, a quadrimodal malware detection model that incorporates text, image, graph, and audio representations of Windows executables. We demonstrate that multimodal transformer fusion can enhance the performance of Windows malware detectors over that of unimodal and bimodal detectors. Malformer employs a combination of two RoBERTa encoders paired with a modified Vision Transformer for image data, WavLM for audio data, and an adaptive loss-weighting scheme to fuse modality-specific representations. Evaluated on a dataset of 201,549 binary samples, Malformer achieved 98.3% accuracy and an F1 score of 0.9833, outperforming both unimodal baselines and bimodal detectors by 4.6-17.6 percentage points. Malformer demonstrates that multimodal fusion provides a promising foundation for countering the growing scale of malware threats, equipping defenders with generalized and resilient detection capabilities.

</details>


### [305] [Toward Quantum Advantage in Learning Parities with Structured Noise via Lower Bound Optimization of the Condition Number](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19122)
*Yusen Han,Xuelian Li,Juntao Gao,Bo Song*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对Macaulay线性系统的条件数优化归约方法，用于高效求解带结构噪声的奇偶校验学习问题（LPSN），通过降低条件数下界来优化量子算法的时间复杂度，并证明在特定参数区间内量子算法优于经典算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LPSN问题可归约为非线性布尔系统求解，在量子计算中通常转化为Macaulay线性系统并通过量子线性系统算法求解，但这一过程严重受限于条件数（condition number）。当前方法因条件数过大而导致量子算法效率低下，亟需一种新的归约方法来克服这一瓶颈。

Method: 作者提出了一种新颖的Macaulay线性系统归约方法：在Ding等人假设下，推导了包含缩放因子的条件数下界；该归约保证量子态高效制备，并通过优化条件数区间来降低条件数下界，从而优化量子算法的时间复杂度上界。此外，利用Macaulay系统的解结构显著降低了LPSN的样本复杂度，并进一步给出了逻辑层面的量子资源估计（电路宽度、深度和门数量）。

Result: 实验结果表明：优化后的条件数直接转化为电路宽度、深度和门数量的降低；通过系统对比量子与经典方法在噪声模式适应性、样本复杂度和时间复杂度方面的表现，证明了在特定参数区间下量子算法具备超越经典算法的潜力。

Conclusion: 本文通过条件数归约优化了量子求解布尔系统的效率，在LPSN问题上同时降低了时间和样本复杂度，并通过资源估计和算法对比验证了量子算法在特定参数区间的优势，为量子密码学中LPSN类问题的求解提供了新的高效路径。

Abstract: Learning Parities with Structured Noise (LPSN) can be reduced to solving nonlinear Boolean systems. In quantum computing, such systems are typically transformed into Macaulay linear systems and solved via quantum linear system algorithms, a process severely limited by the condition number. To address this, we propose a novel reduction method for Macaulay linear systems. Under the assumptions of Ding et al., we derive a condition number lower bound incorporating a scaling factor.
  This reduction not only guarantees efficient quantum state preparation but also exhibits a distinct advantage regarding the condition number interval relative to the reduced right-hand side vector, thereby reducing the lower bound of the condition number and ultimately optimizing the upper bound on the time complexity of the quantum algorithm for solving Boolean systems.
  Furthermore, applying this improved quantum algorithm to LPSN significantly reduces sample complexity by exploiting the Macaulay system's solution structure. We further provide a concrete logical-level quantum resource estimate, demonstrating that the optimized condition number translates directly into a reduction in circuit width, depth, and gate count. Finally, we establish an algorithm selection strategy by systematically comparing quantum and classical approaches across noise pattern adaptability, sample complexity, and time complexity. Results demonstrate that our quantum algorithm exhibits the potential to outperform classical counterparts under specific parameter regimes.

</details>


### [306] [FedGuard-DC: Privacy-Preserving Federated Load Forecasting and Cyber-Attack Detection for Data-Center Loads in Transmission Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19155)
*Md Kibria Saroare,Md Rubel Ahmed*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出FedGuard-DC，一个基于联邦学习的隐私保护框架，用于数据中心负荷预测与本地虚假数据注入攻击（FDIA）检测。每个数据中心在本地训练双头模型（共享编码器+预测头+重构头），仅共享模型更新而不共享原始MW测量数据，并通过校准的异常得分检测被篡改的测量数据。在IEEE 39节点系统中对4个150-350 MW数据中心负荷进行验证，预测RMSE为0.023-0.038 pu，FDIA检测ROC-AUC达0.979。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型数据中心负荷快速增长给电力系统可见性、隐私和网络物理安全带来挑战。系统运营商需要准确的短期负荷预测信息，但数据中心运营商因担心泄露敏感的工作负载和利用模式而不愿共享原始兆瓦级测量数据。

Method: FedGuard-DC采用联邦学习框架：每个数据中心本地训练一个双头模型，包含共享编码器、预测头和重构头；通过结合预测残差和重构误差的校准异常得分进行本地FDIA检测；原始测量数据和绝对MW需求保留在本地，仅将模型更新共享给全局控制器；包含可选的差分隐私和鲁棒截尾均值聚合以评估隐私-效用权衡和恶意客户端鲁棒性；使用EMT仿真数据在IEEE 39节点系统中验证。

Result: 在4个150-350 MW数据中心负荷的验证中，0.5秒超前归一化预测RMSE为0.023-0.038 pu（对比持久法预测的0.32-0.34 pu）；FDIA检测达到ROC-AUC 0.979，F1=0.930，精确率0.988；鲁棒聚合将恶意客户端场景下的RMSE影响从0.042降至0.035 pu。

Conclusion: FedGuard-DC在保护数据中心隐私的同时实现了高精度的短时负荷预测和有效的FDIA检测，展示了联邦学习在电力系统分布式场景下的可行性，为数据中心与系统运营商之间的隐私保护协作提供了有效方案。

Abstract: The rapid growth of large data-center (DC) loads is creating new challenges for power-system visibility, privacy, and cyber-physical security. System operators need accurate short-term information about these fast-varying loads, while DC operators may avoid sharing raw megawatt measurements because they can reveal sensitive workload and utilization patterns. This paper presents FedGuard-DC, a federated learning (FL) framework for privacy-preserving DC load forecasting and local false-data-injection attack (FDIA) detection. Each DC trains a dual-head model on its own measurements, where a shared encoder supports both a forecasting head and a reconstruction head. A calibrated anomaly score combines forecast residual and reconstruction error to detect corrupted measurements locally. Raw measurements and absolute MW demand remain at each DC, while only model updates are shared with the global controller. Optional differential privacy and robust trimmed-mean aggregation are included to evaluate privacy-utility behavior and poisoned-client resilience. The framework is validated using EMT simulation data from four large DC loads rated between 150 and 350 MW integrated into the IEEE 39-bus New England system. Results show a 0.5 s-ahead normalized forecast RMSE of 0.023-0.038 pu, compared with 0.32-0.34 pu for persistence. FedGuard-DC detects FDIA with ROC-AUC of 0.979, F1 = 0.930, and precision of 0.988, while robust aggregation reduces the poisoned-client RMSE impact from 0.042 to 0.035 pu.

</details>


### [307] [SiNMULI: Novel Signed Network Approach for Malicious URL Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19190)
*Avijit Gayen,Sayan Mondal,Angshuman Jana*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SiNMULI，一种基于符号网络的恶意URL识别方法，将有害URL检测建模为符号网络上的二分类问题，利用社交平衡理论的推理机制传播边符号并通过51%多数投票规则分类未标记域，在真实数据集上达到99.89%的准确率，优于传统机器学习和深度学习基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习、深度学习和仿冒网站检测方法主要依赖静态数据分析，难以应对恶意在线实体不断演化的特性，导致检测效果不佳。因此需要一种更具适应性、可解释性且不依赖训练数据的方法来识别恶意URL。

Method: 将恶意URL识别建模为基于符号网络的二分类问题，结合社交网络分析和社交平衡理论。构建以URL为节点、外部超链接（backlinks）为符号边的符号网络，通过平衡理论推理机制传播边符号，并采用51%多数规则对未标记域进行分类。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上，SiNMULI实现了99.89%的准确率、99.62%的精确率和99.80%的F1分数，优于传统机器学习和深度学习的基线模型。此外，该方法具有可解释性、对抗混淆鲁棒性和对训练数据的独立性。

Conclusion: SiNMULI是一种轻量、可扩展且有效的恶意URL识别解决方案，不仅准确率高，还具备可解释性、抗对抗攻击能力以及不依赖训练数据的特性，适合实际网络防御场景。

Abstract: In today's era of rapid advancements in artificial intelligence, computer security and online safeguarding measures have undergone significant improvements. However, malicious websites continue to facilitate the spread of phishing schemes, fraudulent activities and unsolicited communications. Conventional methodologies in machine learning, deep learning and counterfeit website detection predominantly depend on static data analysis, which frequently proves ineffective against the evolving nature of malicious online entities. In response to these challenges, in this work, we propose a signed network-based approach for malicious URL identification, SiNMULI. We introduce an innovative framework that conceptualises the identification of harmful URLs as a signed network-based binary classification problem strongly rooted in the fundamental principles of social network analysis and social balance theory. In this approach, a signed network is constructed based on the backlinks, i.e., external hyperlinks of URLs, wherein each node symbolises a URL and the hyperlinks function as signed edges. Utilising a balance-theoretic inference mechanism, our methodology propagates edge signs and classifies unlabeled domains by employing a 51% majority rule across incoming links. Experimental results on this real-world dataset demonstrate that SiNMULI achieves 99.89% accuracy, 99.62% precision, and 99.80% F1-score, outperforming traditional ML and deep learning baseline models. Beyond high accuracy, SiNMULI offers interpretability, resilience against adversarial obfuscation, and independence from training data, making it a lightweight and scalable solution for real-world cyber defence.

</details>


### [308] [The Structured Totient Preimage Problem: Reconstruction, Collisions, and Cryptographic Implications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19191)
*Luis Adrián Lizama-Pérez*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出并研究结构化欧拉函数原像（STP）问题——给定乘积x=∏(p_i−1)及参数(k,λ)，重构k个不同的λ比特素数。论文给出分解x下有序指数分配计数与多项式重建条件、穷举重建/碰撞算法，以及28组参数（2≤k≤5，λ≤16，最大普查含4,588,935个素数集）的实证分析，量化了非单射性。基于此提出结构化欧拉函数原像假设（STPA），使STP成为候选原像抗性关系，可用于承诺、乘法见证知识证明和认证，但不声称安全归约或后量子硬度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧拉函数反演（inverse-totient）问题在密码学中具有重要意义，但一般形式的重构复杂度尚不明确。作者希望定义一个带结构化约束的特殊反演问题（STP），既能保持可高效验证性，又可能在特定参数区间提供足够困难性，从而为承诺方案、知识证明和认证系统提供新的原像抗性构建基础。核心动机是探索一种可精确刻画、参数可控的密码学难题假设。

Method: 论文从三个层面展开：（1）理论分析：对于分解后的x，推导有序指数分配的确切计数，并证明当k固定且Ω(x)=O(logλ)时，直接重构是多项式时间的，从而排除该参数区间作为强困难性基础；（2）算法设计：给出完整的穷举重构算法和碰撞分析算法；（3）实证研究：对28组参数对（2≤k≤5，λ≤16）进行全量枚举普查，最大规模普查包含4,588,935个素数集，量化碰撞参与率、最大重数和条件模糊度（以比特计）。

Result: 理论结果给出了分解x下重构复杂度的精确刻画；算法方面提供了可操作的穷举重构与碰撞分析工具；实证方面通过大规模普查量化了STP的非单射性。数据表明STP与一般逆欧拉函数计算在结构上可分离，为特定增长参数家族上提出Structured Totient Preimage Assumption（STPA）提供了依据。

Conclusion: STP作为一个受限重构关系，其困难性依赖于具体参数家族的选择，需要专门的STPA假设。在该假设下，STP可作为候选原像抗性关系支撑承诺、乘法见证知识证明和认证应用。论文明确不做安全归约证明，也不主张后量子安全性，其贡献在于确立计算基础和参数约束。

Abstract: We define and study the Structured Totient Preimage (STP) problem as a restricted reconstruction relation with a direct cryptographic motivation. Let $p_1,\ldots,p_k$ be distinct primes of the same bit length and reveal only $x=\prod_{i=1}^k(p_i-1)$. Given $(x,λ,k)$, STP asks for any set of $k$ distinct $λ$-bit primes satisfying this product. The relation is efficiently verifiable, but its reconstruction complexity is not known. We establish three concrete results. First, for factored $x$ we derive the exact number of ordered exponent allocations and a bound showing that direct reconstruction is polynomial for fixed $k$ when $Ω(x)=O(\logλ)$; this rules out that regime as a basis for a strong hardness claim. Second, we give exhaustive algorithms for reconstruction and collision analysis. Third, we exhaustively evaluate 28 parameter pairs, with $2\leq k\leq5$, up to $λ=16$ for pairs and 4,588,935 prime sets in the largest census. The data quantify non-injectivity through collision participation, maximum multiplicity, and conditional ambiguity in bits. These results isolate STP from general inverse-totient computation and motivate a Structured Totient Preimage Assumption for explicitly growing parameter families. Under such an assumption, STP becomes a candidate preimage-resistant relation whose implications for commitments, proofs of knowledge of multiplicative witnesses, and authentication can be stated precisely. The paper establishes the computational foundation and parameter constraints for those constructions; it does not claim a security reduction or post-quantum hardness.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [309] [Optimizing Polynomial Multiplication and Fixed-Weight Sampling for HQC on ARM Cortex-M4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18145)
*Jihoon Jang,Hanbeom Shin,Suhri Kim,Seokhie Hong,Donggeun Kwon*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文在ARM Cortex-M4上实现了优化版Hamming Quasi-Cyclic（HQC）密码方案，通过优化多项式乘法（针对FAFFT蝴蝶运算中的VMOV指令提出脏感知寄存器分配策略和XOR操作重排序）、固定权重采样的支持扩展（谓词执行与4路展开），以及可选缓存策略（复用固定密钥下的公开变换与哈希），在NUCLEO-L4R5ZI板上将密钥生成、封装和拆封性能分别最高提升33.1%、34.6%和29.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HQC作为NIST后量子密码标准化候选方案，在嵌入式平台（特别是ARM Cortex-M4）上的高效实现至关重要。现有实现在多项式乘法的FAFFT蝴蝶计算中，大量指令消耗在VMOV数据搬运而非实际算术运算，且最小化XOR数量不一定能降低总指令数，因此需要更精细的寄存器分配与指令调度优化。

Method: （i）针对FAFFT多项式乘法提出脏感知寄存器分配策略和XOR操作重排序，在不改变XOR数量的情况下将VMOV指令减少最多48.1%；结合FAFFT-CRT方法，并为HQC-1找到比现有模数稀疏34%的FAFFT模数以降低CRT重构成本。（ii）用谓词执行和4路展开重写固定权重采样的支持扩展，将其内循环每字成本从22个周期降至6个周期，同时保持常数时间。（iii）提出可选缓存策略，在固定密钥下复用公开变换和重算的哈希。

Result: 在NUCLEO-L4R5ZI板上，相较两个先前最优实现中较快者，密钥生成、封装和拆封分别最高提升33.1%、34.6%和29.8%；可选缓存策略进一步为封装和拆封带来最高32.7%和18.9%的额外加速。关键优化指标：VMOV指令减少48.1%（XOR数量不变），HQC-1的FAFFT模数稀疏度提升34%，支持扩展内循环从22周期/字降至6周期/字。

Conclusion: 通过针对ARM Cortex-M4微架构特征（特别是杂用寄存器与XOR/VMOV指令平衡）的精细优化，以及常数时间保证下的算法重写和缓存策略，HQC在嵌入式平台上的性能得到显著提升，展示了算法级与指令级协同优化的价值。

Abstract: In this paper, we present an optimized implementation of Hamming Quasi-Cyclic (HQC) on the ARM Cortex-M4. We optimize (i) the polynomial multiplication and (ii) the support expansion in fixed-weight sampling, and (iii) propose an optional caching strategy that reuses the public transforms and hash recomputed under a fixed key. For the polynomial multiplication, the fixed-constant multiplications in the Frobenius additive FFT (FAFFT) butterfly spend nearly half of their instructions on VMOV data movements between general-purpose and floating-point registers rather than arithmetic. Because minimizing the XOR count alone can increase the total instruction count, we propose a dirty-aware register-allocation policy and an XOR-operation reordering that reduce the VMOV count by up to 48.1% while leaving the XOR count unchanged. We apply these to a multiplication that combines prior FAFFT-CRT methods, and for HQC-1 we further find a 34% sparser FAFFT modulus that lowers the CRT reconstruction cost. For fixed-weight sampling, we rewrite the support expansion with predicated execution and 4-way unrolling, lowering the per-word cost of its inner loop from 22 to 6 cycles while remaining constant-time. On the NUCLEO-L4R5ZI board, our implementation reduces key generation, encapsulation, and decapsulation by up to 33.1%, 34.6%, and 29.8% over the faster of the two prior state-of-the-art implementations, and the optional caching yields a further reduction of up to 32.7% and 18.9% for encapsulation and decapsulation.

</details>


### [310] [TokenPowerSandbox: Evidence-Gated CPU-First Screening for Energy-Aware LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18149)
*Chenxu Niu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: TokenPowerSandbox是一个基于证据门控的能源感知LLM服务配置评估工作流，结合可解释的CPU驻留投影器、目标GPU短探测、全负载验证及防篡改的冻结前测量溯源。在NVIDIA H100 80GB上运行Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct，能源MAPE达6.23%和7.35%，但延迟门控揭示了能源精度不能保证延迟性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能源感知的LLM服务需要在真实请求形状下比较不同配置，但穷举式目标GPU性能剖析成本高昂，而廉价预测器在其测量范围外会产生过度的置信度，从而带来风险。

Method: 提出证据门控工作流，包含四个组件：(1)可解释的CPU驻留投影器用于低成本预测；(2)短时间目标GPU探测校准；(3)全工作负载验证；(4)防篡改的"冻结前测量"溯源机制。采用锚定重复与开发负载校准工作负载迁移，冻结模型后在盲测子集和独立预声明的不重拟合确认集上进行评估。

Result: 在NVIDIA H100 80GB上服务Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct（使用vLLM），51次冻结后运行的能源MAPE为6.23%和7.35%，Spearman秩相关系数分别为0.976和0.933。但预先声明的TTFT门控在并发度为4时通过（9.27% MAPE），在并发度低于4时触发弃权（64.80% MAPE）。

Conclusion: 该工作流通过证据门控机制防止模型在其测量范围外过度自信地预测；同时展示能源精度不能作为延迟（TTFT）性能的代理指标，需要在延迟和能源维度分别设置门控。

Abstract: Energy-aware LLM serving requires comparing configurations under realistic request shapes, yet exhaustive target-GPU profiling is costly and a cheap predictor can be dangerously confident outside its measured scope. We present TokenPowerSandbox, an evidence-gated workflow that combines an interpretable CPU-resident projector, short target-GPU probes, full-workload verification, and tamper-evident freeze-before-measurement provenance. On one NVIDIA H100 80GB serving Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct with vLLM, three anchor repeats and six development workloads calibrate workload transfer. The same frozen model is evaluated on a blind holdout and a separately predeclared no-refit confirmation totaling 51 post-freeze runs. Energy MAPE is 6.23% and 7.35%, with Spearman rank correlations of 0.976 and 0.933. However, a predeclared TTFT gate passes at concurrency four (9.27% MAPE) and triggers abstention below four (64.80%), showing why energy accuracy cannot certify latency.

</details>


### [311] [A Fault-Tolerant Spike-Time Interface for Approximate Agreement in Distributed Neuromorphic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18151)
*Arman Ferdowsi,Maryam DehghanChenary,Kevin Tierney,Atakan Aral*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出SpikeTrim算法，解决大规模神经形态系统中多处理瓦片在拜占庭故障下共享控制参数（如阈值参考）的一致性问题。通过SIF接口（分步纪元、发送者归属、首脉冲准入、有界时序误差、静默哨兵）处理仅携带带标签尖峰时间的通信约束，并结合经典MSR规则实现鲁棒近似一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模神经形态系统中多个处理瓦片复制共享控制参数（如阈值参考），若副本发散则相同输入会在不同设定下被处理。然而原始事件流无法满足经典近似一致所需的每发送者一值输入，因为故障发送者可保持静默、淹没接收者或向不同接收者报告不同时间。因此需要为这一受限通信模型设计新的容错一致机制。

Method: 提出SIF（Spike-time Interface for Faults）接口，包含四个关键组件：分步纪元（paced epochs）、发送者归属（sender attribution）、每标签首脉冲准入（per-label FirstSpike admission）、有界时序误差（bounded timing error）和静默哨兵（silence sentinel）。对仿射单脉冲编码采用中点解码获得精确确定性极小极大误差ρ=min{1/2,ω/L}。SpikeTrim将经典均值子序列缩减（MSR）规则应用于发送者索引的解码值，在n≥3f+1条件下保证单步鲁棒有效性。

Result: SpikeTrim在n≥3f+1时保证：单步鲁棒有效性、直接更新下紧密无噪声收缩因子f/(n-2f)、显式最坏情况渐近分歧界、瞬态一致状态损坏后的有限恢复。提供闭式测试判断经验证的时序预算是否满足目标分歧。仿真验证了故障阈值、时序依赖、抗洪泛能力和恢复能力；受控尖峰分类实验显示更快的控制状态对齐与更低预测分歧之间存在关联（在有限维护预算下）。

Conclusion: 本文为神经形态系统中拜占庭容错的控制参数对齐提供了首个系统化方法，将经典MSR鲁棒一致算法适配到仅携带尖峰时间消息的受限通信模型，并给出了精确的误差界、收缩因子和恢复保证，理论与仿真实验均验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: Large neuromorphic systems contain many processing tiles that may replicate a shared control parameter such as a threshold reference. If these copies diverge, identical inputs may be processed under different intended settings. We study how tiles can reduce this disagreement when communication carries only labeled spike times and up to \(f\) sender labels may be Byzantine. A raw event stream cannot supply the one-value-per-sender input required by classical approximate agreement because a faulty sender can remain silent, flood a receiver, or report different times to different receivers.
  We introduce the Spike-time Interface for Faults, or \SIF, which combines paced epochs, sender attribution, per-label \FirstSpike admission, bounded timing error, and a silence sentinel. For an affine one-spike code, midpoint decoding attains the exact deterministic minimax error \(ρ=\min\{1/2,ω/L\}\), where \(ω\) is the residual timing uncertainty and \(L\) is the usable encoding window. \SpikeTrim applies the classical mean-subsequence-reduced (MSR) rule to the sender-indexed decoded values. For \(n\ge3f+1\), it guarantees one-step robust validity, the tight noiseless contraction factor \(f/(n-2f)\) under direct updates, an explicit worst-case asymptotic disagreement bound, and finite recovery after transient agreement-state corruption. A closed-form test determines whether a validated timing budget meets a target disagreement. Simulations illustrate the fault threshold, timing dependence, flooding resistance, and recovery. A controlled spiking classifier experiment shows an association between faster control-state alignment and lower prediction disagreement under a finite maintenance budget.

</details>


### [312] [FPGA Lifecycle Management for RISC-V Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18156)
*Tianhai Liu,James J. Hunt*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种主机无关的FPGA生命周期管理架构，将控制平面从专有工具链和特定主机架构中解耦，通过利用标准Linux能力将生命周期管理提升到操作系统层，使支持Linux的RISC-V处理器可作为异构FPGA系统中的控制主机。在Zynq-7000 SoC上完成原型验证，可泛化至RISC-V平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FPGA生命周期管理仍受限于专有工具链和主机架构，RISC-V缺乏供应商中立的位流部署规模化模型，现有方案无法实现异构FPGA系统中的可移植部署。

Method: 提出一种主机无关的控制平面架构，将生命周期管理下沉至操作系统层，利用标准Linux能力代替专有厂商栈，从而将部署与特定ISA和供应商工具链解耦。

Result: 在Zynq-7000 SoC上完成原型实现，验证该架构可推广至RISC-V平台，为大规模FPGA集群管理提供可移植的基础。

Conclusion: 该架构实现了FPGA生命周期管理与具体ISA和供应商栈的解耦，为面向RISC-V的异构FPGA系统规模化位流部署提供了供应商中立的可移植方案。

Abstract: FPGA lifecycle management remains tied to proprietary toolchains and host architectures, leaving RISC-V without a vendor-neutral model for scalable bitstream deployment. A host-agnostic control-plane architecture is presented that shifts lifecycle management to the operating-system layer by leveraging standard Linux capabilities, thereby decoupling deployment from specific ISAs and vendor stacks. This enables Linux-capable RISC-V processors to serve as control hosts in heterogeneous FPGA systems. Prototyped on a Zynq-7000 SoC and generalizable to RISC-V platforms, the architecture provides a portable foundation for fleet-scale FPGA management.

</details>


### [313] [CTTE: An Open Dual-Protocol RISC-V Trace Encoder for N-Trace and E-Trace](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18170)
*Alexander Weiss,Albert Schulz*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出CTTE（CEDARtools.TraceEncoder），一个开源的SystemVerilog处理器跟踪编码器，具备协议无关的前端和可选的N-Trace/Nexus或E-Trace后端。CTTE是首个同时支持两种RISC-V跟踪格式且共享统一前端的可综合硬件编码器，已在六个来自五家供应商的RISC-V核上集成验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RISC-V标准定义了两种处理器跟踪格式（N-Trace和E-Trace），它们共享hart到编码器的指令跟踪接口，但在压缩、消息和帧格式上有所不同。截至2026年8月，没有公开可综合的N-Trace编码器，也没有同时具备两个后端并共享统一前端的硬件编码器被报道，存在明显的技术空白。

Method: CTTE采用协议无关的前端架构和可选择的N-Trace/Nexus或E-Trace后端。实现了N-Trace 1.0程序跟踪消息集（两种指令跟踪模式），支持参数化的N-Trace地址宽度，遵循RISC-V Trace Control Interface。验证方法结合指令精确往返测试、RTL不变量、形式化模型检查和带负对照的机器评判硬件测试。集成六个来自五家供应商的RISC-V核，并在64位RISC-V系统上引导Linux进行评估。

Result: 在64位RISC-V系统上成功引导Linux（包括双hart SMP系统），演示了源侧进程上下文过滤可将目标进程在固定缓冲区中的观测深度翻倍。硬件和配置保持不变时，工作负载选择使跟踪成本从0.21到4.90 bits/条退役指令变化。统一前端实现了可控的后端比较，同时暴露了必须与线格式效应分离的同步和传输效应。

Conclusion: CTTE填补了RISC-V领域缺乏同时支持N-Trace和E-Trace的公开可综合硬件编码器的空白。开源发布（CERN-OHL-S-2.0）包括RTL、寄存器源、测试、形式化属性、解码器扩展和构建脚本；同时提供Accemic商业许可下的专有集成方案。

Abstract: RISC-V standardizes two processor-trace formats, N-Trace and E-Trace, that share a hart-to-encoder instruction trace interface but differ in compression, messages, and framing. To the best of our knowledge, as of August 2026, no publicly available synthesizable N-Trace encoder and no published hardware encoder with both back ends behind a common front end have been reported. We present CTTE (CEDARtools.TraceEncoder), an open SystemVerilog encoder with a protocol-agnostic front end and selectable N-Trace/Nexus or E-Trace back ends. CTTE implements the N-Trace 1.0 program-trace message set in both instruction-trace modes, supports parameterized N-Trace address width, follows the RISC-V Trace Control Interface, and announces output-bandwidth loss before state re-convergence on the evaluated N-Trace path. CTTE has been integrated with six RISC-V cores from five suppliers. We evaluate 64-bit RISC-V systems booting Linux, including a two-hart SMP system, and demonstrate source-side process-context filtering that doubles observation depth for a target process in a fixed buffer. Verification combines instruction-exact round trips, RTL invariants, formal model checking, and machine-judged hardware campaigns with negative controls. With hardware and configuration held constant, workload choice changes trace cost from 0.21 to 4.90 bits per retired instruction. A common front end also enables controlled back-end comparisons while exposing synchronization and transport effects that must be separated from wire-format effects. RTL, register sources, tests, formal properties, decoder extensions, and build scripts are released under CERN-OHL-S-2.0; proprietary integration is available under an Accemic commercial license.

</details>


### [314] [MAGMA: Mixture-Model Adaptive Gaussian Model Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18366)
*Peter Forcha,Harshitha Kajekusumadhar,Mbua Peter,Muhammed Kawser,Audrey Cyriell Mo*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: MAGMA是一个完全可综合的定点FPGA架构，在边缘FPGA上实现GMM的并发推理与在线EM参数自适应，通过硬件友好的超越函数近似和数值稳定性保护机制，以有限的资源开销（7,779 LUTs、91 DSPs、无BRAM）在AMD Spartan-7上以74.49MHz运行，相比软件实现获得11.8倍推理加速和81倍M-step加速，并在非平稳场景下优于静态基线（81.5% vs 79.7%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统FPGA上的GMM加速器使用离线训练、固定参数，无法在长期运行的边缘系统中适应不断变化的场景统计特性，缺乏在线自适应的能力。

Method: 提出MAGMA架构，包含：(1) 流水线化的GMM推理数据通路；(2) 基于在线EM的背景更新引擎，采用硬件友好的超越函数近似（区间缩减的Chebyshev指数、基于CLZ的对数、移位-减法除法）；(3) 针对方差坍缩和簇死亡的数值稳定保护机制。通过空间子采样降低每更新像素量40倍，同时保持EM收敛性能。

Result: 在AMD Spartan-7 XC7S50上以K=4个簇实现，运行频率74.49MHz，占用7,779个LUT、91个DSP、无块RAM，功耗274mW。相对软件实现获得11.8倍推理加速和81倍M-step加速；空间子采样减少40倍更新像素量且对EM收敛影响极小；在合成非平稳场景中，在线自适应的平均像素精度为81.5%，优于静态基线的79.7%。

Conclusion: 证明了在商品级边缘FPGA上完全实现在线GMM学习是可行的，为长期运行边缘系统中自适应的计算机视觉处理提供了高效的硬件解决方案。

Abstract: Conventional FPGA-based Gaussian Mixture Model (GMM) accelerators use offline-trained, fixed parameters, limiting their ability to adapt to evolving scene statistics in long-lived edge systems. We present MAGMA, a fully synthesizable fixed-point FPGA architecture that performs concurrent GMM inference and online Expectation-Maximization (EM) parameter adaptation from a streaming RGB pixel input. MAGMA combines a pipelined inference datapath with a background update engine using hardware-friendly transcendental approximations---a range-reduced Chebyshev exponential, a CLZ-based logarithm, and a shift-and-subtract divider---alongside guards against variance collapse and cluster death that stabilize online fixed-point EM. Implemented on an AMD Spartan-7 XC7S50 with $K=4$ clusters, MAGMA runs at 74.49~MHz using 7,779 LUTs, 91 DSPs, and no block RAM, consuming 274~mW. It achieves an $11.8\times$ inference speedup and an $81\times$ M-step speedup over software, while spatial subsampling reduces per-update pixel volume by $40\times$ with minimal impact on EM convergence. Under a synthetic non-stationary scene, MAGMA's online adaptation improves mean pixel accuracy over a static baseline (81.5\% vs.\ 79.7\%), demonstrating that full online GMM learning is achievable on a commodity edge FPGA.

</details>


### [315] [Coverage-Driven RTL Assertion Generation with Formal Exploration and Neuro-Symbolic Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18482)
*Zhiyuan Yan,Ziyue Zheng,Hongce Zhang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: NeuroAssertion是一个覆盖驱动的断言生成框架，结合形式化迹生成、语法引导合成(SyGuS)和智能体式精化过程，通过模型检查生成行为多样化的迹，利用LLM提出候选断言并在失败时通过神经符号修复过程生成修复语法，最终比传统方法多约2倍断言并提高约2倍突变覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的断言挖掘方法存在两大问题：(1) 随机或有限的迹无法覆盖难以到达的行为；(2) 一次性生成缺乏反馈机制，无法告知用户哪些行为仍未被验证或断言集应如何改进。这导致即使生成了大量断言，关键设计行为仍可能未被覆盖。

Method: 该框架包含三个核心步骤：(1) 将难以到达的控制流条件转化为形式化可达性目标，使用模型检查生成行为多样化的迹；(2) 从这些迹中利用语法引导合成(SyGuS)挖掘初始断言；(3) 在验证反馈下进行智能体式精化：一个LLM对未覆盖区域提出候选断言，若候选断言无法通过形式化检查，则第二个LLM生成修复语法，指导约束符号合成完成神经符号修复。

Result: 实验结果表明，NeuroAssertion相比传统断言挖掘方法能够生成约2倍的断言数量，并实现约2倍的突变覆盖率提升。

Conclusion: NeuroAssertion通过将形式化方法、符号合成与大语言模型相结合，构建了一个闭环的覆盖驱动断言生成框架，能够有效提高断言的完整性和可靠性，显著改善硬件功能验证中的断言覆盖效果。

Abstract: Hardware functional verification relies on high-quality assertions to expose design bugs and establish confidence in Register Transfer Level (RTL) designs. Yet existing assertion mining methods still struggle to produce complete and reliable assertion sets: random or limited traces fail to cover hard-to-reach behaviors, and one-shot generation provides little feedback about what remains unverified or how the assertion set should be improved. As a result, critical design behaviors can remain uncovered even when many assertions are generated. We present NeuroAssertion, a coverage-driven assertion generation framework that combines formal trace generation, syntax-guided synthesis (SyGuS), and an agent-inspired refinement process within a unified framework. Our framework first converts hard-to-reach control-flow conditions into formal reachability objectives, uses model checking to generate behaviorally diverse traces, and mines initial assertions from these traces with SyGuS. It then performs targeted agent-inspired refinement under verification feedback: one LLM first proposes candidate assertions for uncovered regions, and if a candidate fails formal checking, a second LLM generates a repair grammar that guides constrained symbolic synthesis in a neuro-symbolic repair procedure. Experimental results show that this framework delivers around 2X more assertions and about 2X higher mutation coverage than traditional assertion mining methods.

</details>


### [316] [APEX: A Dual-Sparsity Accelerator for Precise and Efficient SNN Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19046)
*Devgokul Bawa Venkatesh,Sreeram Radhakrishnan,Rajshekhar Rakshit,Gopalakrishnan Srinivasan*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: APEX是一个集成PASC-IF神经元到LoAS硬件框架中的双稀疏SNN推理加速器，通过全组合逻辑实现PASC-IF数据路径且无额外延迟开销，在保持ANN等效精度的同时显著减少推理时间步，能耗降低40%，精度提升最高3%，功耗开销仅1.3%-5.4%，面积开销2.1%-2.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SNN作为ANN的能效替代方案备受关注，ANN-SNN转换（特别是QCFS激活）虽然能实现高精度，但需要大量推理时间步才能匹配源ANN精度。PASCAL提出的PASC-IF神经元虽在算法层面实现了数学等价性并减少时间步，但其硬件部署影响尚未被探索，缺乏针对该神经元的专用硬件加速器设计。

Method: 提出APEX加速器，将PASC-IF神经元集成到LoAS硬件框架中。采用三阶段PASC-IF数据路径的纯组合逻辑实现，无额外延迟开销；通过全时间并行数据流同时利用输入脉冲稀疏性和权重稀疏性（双稀疏），实现高效稀疏计算并减少内存流量。

Result: 在所有评估模型中，PASC-IF神经元相比标准IF神经元平均准确率提升最高达3%，功耗仅增加1.3%-5.4%，面积开销2.1%-2.7%，在最佳精度配置下能耗降低40%。

Conclusion: APEX成功验证了PASC-IF神经元在硬件上的高效可部署性，以极小的功耗和面积代价实现了更高的精度和更少的推理时间步，同时通过双稀疏时间并行架构显著降低能耗，为高精度低时步SNN推理提供了实用的硬件解决方案。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) have emerged as an energy-efficient alternative to Artificial Neural Networks (ANNs), leveraging sparse accumulate operations in the place of power-hungry multiply-and-accumulate operations. ANN-SNN conversion is a widely adopted approach to realize deep SNNs with accuracy comparable to that of ANNs. The Quantization-Clip-Floor-Shift (QCFS) activation minimizes conversion error, yet requires a large number of inference timesteps to match the source ANN accuracy on real-world vision datasets. PASCAL addresses this by proposing the Precise ANN-SNN Conversion Integrate-and-Fire (PASC-IF) neuron, which guarantees mathematical equivalence between the converted SNN and the source ANN, thereby achieving ANN-equivalent accuracy at significantly reduced timesteps. Despite this algorithmic advancement, the hardware implications of deploying the PASC-IF neuron remain unexplored. In this work, we present APEX, a dual-sparsity SNN inference accelerator that integrates the PASC-IF neuron into the LoAS hardware framework. The three-stage PASC-IF datapath is realized as a fully combinational circuit with no additional latency cost. APEX exploits dual sparsity in both input spikes and weights through a fully temporal-parallel dataflow, enabling efficient sparse computation and reduced memory traffic. Across all evaluated models, the PASC-IF neuron on average achieves up to 3% higher accuracy than the standard IF neuron, with a power overhead of only 1.3%-5.4%, an area overhead of 2.1%-2.7%, and 40% energy reduction for best accuracy configurations.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [317] [Position: Collusion Risks Among AI Reasoning Agents Justify Certification Requirements for Making Market Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18078)
*Matthew Riemer,Tommaso Tosato,Amin Memarian,Maximilian Puelma Touzel,Glen Berseth,Irina Rish,Guillaume Dumas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出具备链式推理能力的AI智能体天然倾向于合谋行为，应在其做出影响经济市场的决策前获得行为认证。实验表明DeepSeek-R1在Bertrand寡头定价中表现出隐蔽合谋倾向，且其推理轨迹可被隐蔽操控而不被发现，因此需要基于可观察行为的事前认证机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体整合进社会可能瓦解法律上竞争与合谋之间的证据区分，但不消除经济损害方面的区分。推理型智能体的链式思维可能产生隐蔽的合谋行为，缺乏可识别的共谋证据或意图，从而规避现行反垄断法律框架。

Method: 在Bertrand寡头定价领域对DeepSeek-R1智能体进行实验，测试其在标准环境中的合谋倾向、在人类明确提示不合作时的行为，以及通过链式思维操控将智能体导向极端合谋或高度竞争行为的效果，并用另一个LLM评估推理轨迹的语义可检测性。

Result: DeepSeek-R1表现出持续的隐性合谋倾向，即使人类指示不要合谋也不消退；链式思维可被操控至极端合谋或竞争行为，且这种操控对分析推理轨迹的另一个LLM在语义上不可检测；初步证据表明智能体可被泛化地引导向有效竞争均衡。

Conclusion: 必须建立基于代表性情境中可观察行为的行为认证机制来防止合谋。在开发出全面行为认证之前，这些推理型AI模型不应被部署到真实市场环境中，以确保市场的稳定性和效率。

Abstract: This position paper argues that AI agents with chain-of-thought reasoning capabilities are predisposed to exhibit collusive behavior and should be required to obtain behavioral certification before making decisions that affect economic markets. This is because integrating these agents into society could collapse the legal evidentiary distinction between competition and collusion among independent firms without eroding the economic harm distinction. Experiments with DeepSeek-R1 agents in the Bertrand oligopoly pricing domain reveal a tendency towards tacit collusion that persists even when humans prompt the agents not to collude. We further show that the chain-of-thought of these agents can be steered toward either extremely collusive or highly competitive behavior in a way that is not semantically detectable by another LLM analyzing the reasoning traces. As a result, deploying reasoning agents for market decisions leads to collusive economic outcomes without any evidence of conspiracy or intent. Thus, certification based on observed behavior in representative situations is necessary to prevent collusion. We provide preliminary evidence that such agents can be steered in a generalizable way toward efficient competitive equilibria. However, developing a comprehensive behavioral certification will be required before these models can be deployed in real-world markets while ensuring their stability and efficiency.

</details>


### [318] [Position: Profiling Game Worlds by Transition Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18079)
*Lele Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出转换复杂度画像（TCP）——一套小规模、可复现的度量标准，用于量化游戏世界建模（GWM）和强化学习（RL）环境中转换预测问题的难度，涵盖一步分支、交互不确定性、时间/空间依赖跨度等维度，并呼吁将其作为基准元数据和论文必备统计数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GWM与RL研究常因未量化所声明接口（像素/令牌/潜变量及有限历史）下的转换预测问题难度而被混淆，导致不同基准之间的结果难以公平比较。

Method: 定义TCP度量集，包含三类指标：（1）内在一步分支性；（2）交互诱导的不确定性与可观测时的对手影响；（3）通过标准化探针曲线度量的时间/空间依赖跨度。报告需附带明确的参考分布、协议随机性和版本化的测量预算（采样/重采样及固定探针计算量）。

Result: 展示了常见游戏族和现代"神经游戏引擎"领域在TCP景观中的分布位置，论证了TCP能使不同基准间的数值具有可比性。

Conclusion: 呼吁将TCP确立为标准基准元数据，并在GWM与RL论文中作为必报统计量，以提升研究可比性和可复现性。

Abstract: Game world modeling (GWM) and reinforcement learning (RL) are often confounded because research papers rarely quantify how difficult the underlying transition prediction problem is at the declared interface (pixels/tokens/latents with finite history). We propose the Transition Complexity Profile (TCP): a small, reproducible set of metrics that characterizes an environment's (or gameplay dataset's) induced transition kernel by (i) intrinsic one-step branching, (ii) interaction-induced uncertainty and opponent influence when observable, and (iii) temporal/spatial dependency span via standardized probe curves. TCP is reported with an explicit reference distribution, protocol stochasticity, and a versioned measurement budget (sampling/resampling and fixed probe compute), enabling comparable numbers across benchmarks. We outline how common game families and modern "neural game engine" domains populate this landscape and call for TCP to become standard benchmark metadata and a required statistic in GWM and RL papers.

</details>


### [319] [Large Language Models in Mental Health: A Systematic Review of Applications, Innovations, and Ethical Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18080)
*Yisong Chen,Yifan Gao,Sijing Yu,Chuqing Zhao,Yang Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了大语言模型（LLMs）在心理健康领域（而非广义健康领域）的应用，涵盖社交媒体分析、临床对话代理、治疗支持工具、提示工程、多模态学习及伦理考量，并强调其在抑郁症早期检测、自杀风险评估、个性化治疗支持和心理教育内容生成方面的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心理健康问题日益严峻，而大语言模型在心理健康诊断、监测和治疗支持方面展现出巨大潜力。然而该领域跨学科、数据来源多样，缺乏系统性综述来整合现有研究发现和技术进展，因此需要一篇全面回顾来梳理LLMs在心理健康领域的应用现状、技术方法及面临的挑战。

Method: 采用综述方法，整合跨学科研究的发现，涵盖多类数据源（社交媒体帖子、电子病历、多模态输入），从模型进展、标注策略、提示工程、多模态融合技术（文本、语音、传感器数据）以及伦理监管框架等多个维度进行系统性梳理和归纳。

Result: 综述指出LLMs在心理健康领域已实现早期抑郁检测、自杀风险评估、个性化治疗支持和心理教育内容生成等应用；模型和标注策略的进步提升了可解释性和临床相关性；提示工程对领域适配至关重要；多模态融合技术（文本+语音+传感器）可改善心理健康诊断与监测。

Conclusion: 文章强调需正视伦理、社会技术和监管层面的持续挑战，倡导建立确保LLMs在真实心理健康护理中安全、公平和负责任部署的框架体系。

Abstract: We present a review on the applications of large language models (LLMs) in health, e.g., social media analysis, clinical conversational agents, therapy support tools, prompt engineering, multimodal learning, and ethical considerations. We integrate findings from interdisciplinary studies utilizing diverse data sources such as social media posts, electronic medical records, and multimodal inputs to enable early detection of depression, suicide risk assessment, personalized therapy support, and psychoeducational content generation. Our review highlights advancements in LLM models and annotation strategies that enhance interpretability and clinical relevance, while we also emphasize the critical role of prompt engineering for domain adaptation. We also discuss emerging multimodal fusion techniques integrating text, speech, and sensor data for improved mental health diagnosis and monitoring. Finally, we address ongoing ethical, sociotechnical, and regulatory challenges, and advocate frameworks to ensure safe, equitable, and accountable deployment of LLMs in real-world mental health care.

</details>


### [320] [Position: Behavioral Systems Require Behavioral Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18081)
*Manuel Cherep,Nikhil Singh,Pattie Maes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张AI智能体应像其他行为系统一样被评估，通过系统观察、扰动和解释其行为过程，而非仅关注性能结果，并提出发展严格行为测试的研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI智能体的评估方法主要聚焦于性能结果（performance outcomes），而忽略了产生这些结果的行为过程（behavioral processes）。由于智能体越来越多地作为行为系统与动态环境交互、追求目标并随时间适应，仅以输出结果评估不足以理解其内在决策机制和行为模式，因此需要借鉴行为科学的评估范式。

Method: 作者借鉴行为科学的方法论，提出研究议程，包括三个核心方向：（1）从动作序列中恢复决策策略（recovering decision strategies）；（2）构建能够隔离行为差异的环境（constructing environments that isolate behavioral differences）；（3）探测多智能体系统中的涌现动力学（probing emergent dynamics）。

Result: 本文是观点性/立场性论文（position paper），本身不提供实证结果，而是提出了一种新的评估范式和相应的前沿研究路线图，为发展"AI行为科学"奠定概念基础。

Conclusion: 作者认为AI智能体应被视为需要行为级评估的系统，通过系统观测、扰动实验和动作解释来理解其行为过程。这些研究方向共同构成了一条发展AI行为科学的路线图，有望改变当前以结果为导向的评估方式。

Abstract: Artificial agentic systems increasingly operate as behavioral systems by interacting with dynamic environments, pursuing goals, and adapting over time. Yet, current evaluation methods largely focus on performance outcomes, not the underlying behavioral processes that produce them. This paper argues that AI agents must be evaluated like other behavioral systems: through systematic observation, perturbation, and interpretation of their actions. We draw on lessons from the behavioral sciences to motivate this position, and propose a research agenda focused on developing rigorous behavioral tests. These include methods for recovering decision strategies from action sequences, constructing environments that isolate behavioral differences, and probing emergent dynamics in multi-agent systems. Taken together, these directions offer a roadmap for developing a science of AI behavior.

</details>


### [321] [Position: Current Model Cards Are Insufficient for Downstream Governance of Open-Weight Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18086)
*Sungwon Chae,Keonwoo Kim,Hoki Kim,Jaeyeon Ju,Sangchul Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过分析Hugging Face上500个模型卡片(position paper)，主张对开放权重基础模型(OWFMs)的有效治理需要三层互补框架：模型卡片、可接受使用政策(AUPs)和许可证的整合，而非依赖单一透明度工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管模型卡片已被广泛采用为模型仓库的透明度工具，但现有框架未能充分告知下游开发者关于OWFMs的独特安全挑战，存在治理缺口。

Method: 分析Hugging Face上500个模型卡片，识别现有监管方法（包括模型血统、对齐溯源和经验观察行为）留下的安全缺口，并论证标准开源许可证(OSLs)不适合OWFMs且可能削弱AUP的可执行性。

Result: 现有模型卡片缺乏安全性关键信息，标准开源许可证对OWFMs不适用并可能弱化AUP执行力；提出将模型卡片、AUP和许可证演化为集成化安全工件，整合信息、规范和法律三个维度。

Conclusion: OWFMs的有效治理需要超越单一模型卡片的局限，采用模型卡片、AUP和许可证三者协同的多层治理框架，以构建更加全面的安全治理体系。

Abstract: The growth of open-weight foundation models (OWFMs) has prompted the AI community to re-evaluate strategies for effective downstream governance. Although model cards have been widely adopted as transparency artifacts in model repositories, existing frameworks often fail to adequately inform downstream developers and users about the distinct safety challenges posed by OWFMs. This position paper analyzes 500 model cards hosted on Hugging Face and argues that effective governance of OWFMs requires a multi-layered approach integrating three complementary components: (i) model cards, (ii) acceptable use policies (AUPs), and (iii) licenses. To motivate this claim, we identify a safety gap left by existing regulatory approaches, including model heritage, alignment provenance, and empirically observed behaviors, through an analysis of model cards with safety-critical information. We further argue that standard open-source licenses (OSLs) are not well suited for OWFMs and may weaken the enforceability of AUPs. Building on these observations, we outline directions for evolving model cards, AUPs, and licenses into integrated safety artifacts to enable a more comprehensive governance framework that coherently integrates informational, normative, and legal dimensions.

</details>


### [322] [A Metamorphic Artificial Age Score Decision-Support Prototype for Flight-Log-Based Drone Propeller Health Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18088)
*Seyma Yaman Kayadibi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于变形式人工年龄分数（AAS）的决策支持原型，用于无人机螺旋桨飞行日志健康监测。通过从飞行日志中计算六个健康指标（轨迹跟踪误差、姿态不稳定性、推力指令负担等），并将其归一化后通过候选评分策略和冗余调整的AAS公式进行评估，实现了对健康与三种缺陷螺旋桨案例的区分，为飞行后维护优先级排序和自主系统监督提供了多指标决策支持层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机螺旋桨故障的影响可能分布在多个飞行日志通道中而非表现为单一诊断信号，这给安全性和可靠性带来风险。现有方法缺乏能够综合多个指标进行健康监测和维护决策支持的手段。

Method: 使用2024 DronePropA公开数据集中的历史真实飞行日志，从原始MATLAB矩阵计算六个健康相关指标（轨迹跟踪误差、姿态不稳定性、推力指令负担、电机指令不平衡、ESC指令不稳定性和电池电压应力）；这些指标相对于健康基线归一化，通过候选评分策略、变形式充分性关系和冗余调整的AAS公式进行评估。

Result: 在一个健康基线和三个缺陷螺旋桨案例的回顾性评估中：健康案例被分配常规监控；Severity 1案例以ESC指令不稳定性为主，被分配维修审查；Severity 2案例达到最大电机指令和ESC指令负担，Severity 3案例达到最大轨迹跟踪误差，两者均触发强制检查。

Conclusion: 螺旋桨故障效应可能通过不同的运行通道显现，支持建立多指标决策支持层的必要性，用于飞行后维护优先级排序和自主系统监督。

Abstract: Drone propeller faults can create safety and reliability risks when their effects are distributed across multiple flight-log channels rather than appearing as a single diagnostic signal. This paper proposes a Metamorphic Artificial Age Score (AAS) decision-support prototype for flight-log-based drone propeller health monitoring. Using selected historical real flight logs from the 2024 DronePropA public dataset, the framework computes six health-related indicators from raw MATLAB matrices: trajectory tracking error, attitude instability, thrust-command burden, motor-command imbalance, ESC-command instability, and battery-level stress. These indicators are normalized relative to a healthy baseline and evaluated through candidate scoring policies, metamorphic adequacy relations, and a redundancy-adjusted AAS formulation. In this context, AAS is used as a structural policy-adequacy and burden measure rather than as a chronological age measure. A controlled retrospective evaluation was performed using one healthy baseline and three defective propeller cases under the same speed profile and trajectory. The healthy case was assigned to routine monitoring. The Severity 1 case was dominated by ESC-command instability and assigned to maintenance review. The Severity 2 case reached maximum motor-command and ESC-command burden, while the Severity 3 case reached maximum trajectory tracking error; both triggered mandatory inspection. The results show that propeller fault effects may appear through different operational channels, supporting the need for a multi-indicator decision-support layer for post-flight maintenance prioritization and autonomous-system oversight.

</details>


### [323] [Position: Multi-Agent Systems Should Prioritize Concurrency Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18092)
*Xin Yang,Letian Li,Zimo Ji,Terry Jingchen Zhang,Wenyuan Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种观点：基于LLM的多智能体系统（MAS）中的许多失败，本质上是一类并发控制问题——智能体并发读写共享状态，而LLM长推理窗口放大了脏读、丢失更新和不一致结果的风险。作者主张MAS框架应通过显式并发控制机制（冲突检测、隔离保证、结构化共享资源访问）来解决这些失败，而非事后补救。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MAS虽承诺可扩展协作，但添加智能体后可靠性往往下降。现有文献多将这些失败归因于协调或通信问题，本文揭示其深层根源在于并发控制缺陷，从而为系统设计指明正确方向。

Method: 本文为立场论文（position paper），采用概念映射方法：将MAS中常见的失败模式（协调失败、通信中断）系统映射到经典并发异常（脏读、丢失更新、不一致结果），并论证将显式并发控制机制（冲突检测、隔离保证、结构化资源访问）作为MAS框架的一等设计关注点。

Result: 论证了MAS失败可重新归类为经典并发控制问题，并提出并发控制应为MAS框架设计中的一等公民，而非事后附加功能。

Conclusion: MAS框架设计应从关注协调与通信转向显式的并发控制机制，以保障多智能体协作的可靠性与一致性。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) promise scalable collaboration, yet adding agents often reduces reliability. This position paper argues that many MAS failures are fundamentally concurrency control problems: agents concurrently read and write shared state, and long LLM inference windows amplify the risk of stale reads, lost updates, and inconsistent outcomes. Failure modes commonly attributed to coordination or communication breakdowns can be mapped directly onto classical concurrency anomalies. We contend that MAS frameworks should address these failures through explicit concurrency control mechanisms: conflict detection, isolation guarantees, and structured access to shared resources. Concurrency control should be a first-class design concern, not an afterthought.

</details>


### [324] [FinSkillBench: Evaluating AI Agents and Domain Skills for Investment Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18099)
*Jermyn Zhen Yong Bek,Zhuang Qiang Bok,Zhongtian Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FinSkillBench评估套件，用于衡量语言模型代理在投资管理任务中有效使用财务领域技能的能力。基准覆盖投资组合构建、风险管理和基本面分析三个领域，包含12个子任务和2,603个任务片段。研究发现：精心策划的技能包能显著提升性能（平均得分从0.366提高至0.528），而自主生成的技能收益甚微且计算成本更高。跨框架验证（Hermes Agent, 8个模型）复现了相同趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投资管理是高风险的领域，代理式AI系统必须超越生成流畅文本的能力，需要检索时点数据、组装正确的计算输入、调用专业方法并生成可审计的结构化输出。现有评估未能充分衡量语言模型代理在这一专业领域的技能运用能力，因此需要专门的评估基准。

Method: 构建FinSkillBench基准，涵盖投资组合构建、风险管理和基本面分析三个域，含12个子任务和2,603个任务片段。每个片段提供时点输入、隐藏真实答案和任务特定验证器。对比三种条件：无技能、策划技能包（程序文档+可执行组件）、自主生成技能。在9个模型上进行大规模评估，并在独立框架Hermes Agent上用8个模型、5,280个片段进行复现验证。

Result: 策划技能包持续提升性能，将平均得分从0.366提升至0.528，在投资组合构建和风险管理领域增益最大。自主生成技能收益甚微但计算成本更高。独立评估（Hermes Agent框架）在全部三个领域复现了相同的方向性模式，但技能效果的幅度因子任务和框架而异。

Conclusion: 在投资管理代理中，获得可靠的程序化技能与模型选择同样重要，而天真的自主生成技能往往无效。作者发布了基准、评估工具、策划技能包和完整轨迹以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Investment management is a high-stakes domain in which agentic AI systems must do more than generate plausible text. They must retrieve point-in-time data, assemble correct computational inputs, invoke specialized methods, and produce auditable structured outputs. We introduce FinSkillBench, an evaluation suite designed to measure whether language model agents can effectively use financial domain skills to solve investment management tasks. The benchmark spans three domains, portfolio construction, risk management, and fundamental analysis, and includes 12 subtasks with 2,603 task episodes.
  Each episode provides point-in-time inputs, hidden ground truth, and a task-specific verifier.We compare three conditions: no skill, curated skill packages consisting of procedural documents and executable components, and self-generated skills in which the agent writes and reuses its own procedures within an episode. Across 9 models and a large-scale evaluation, curated skills consistently improve performance, raising mean scores from 0.366 to 0.528, with the largest gains in portfolio construction and risk management.
  In contrast, self-generated skills provide little benefit despite higher computational cost. An independent evaluation using a separate agent framework (Hermes Agent, 8 models, 5,280 episodes total) reproduces the directional pattern across all three domains, with the magnitude of skill effects varying by subtask and harness.
  These results showthat in investment management agents, access to reliable procedural skills can be as important as model choice, while naive self-generation of skills is often ineffective. We release the benchmark, evaluation tools, curated skill packages, and full trajectories to support further research.

</details>


### [325] [Self-Evolving Agents as Dynamic Graph Transformation: A Survey and New Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18104)
*Yuanyuan Xu,Wenjie Zhang,Yin Chen,Xuemin Lin,Ying Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本综述提出将LLM智能体的自我演化建模为动态图变换，系统性地将智能体状态（记忆、工具、技能、工作流、智能体间关系）表示为动态图，并构建了四类演化分类体系（节点/特征演化、边/拓扑演化、子图激活、跨组件协同演化），同时从动态图学习视角提出九个子领域映射到智能体演化能力的方法框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图智能体综述将图视为智能体功能的支撑结构而非演化基质，而自我演化智能体综述则聚焦于智能体层面的机制，很少讨论图拓扑的演化。因此，智能体状态演化与动态图拓扑之间的耦合关系尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 将智能体状态建模为动态图，其中记忆、工具、技能、工作流及智能体间关系以带类型的节点、边和子图形式表示，并通过模式约束重写进行更新。基于此，将动态图方法组织为四类演化分类体系，并提出九类动态图学习子领域的映射方法，最后从动态图视角提出五类图感知的评估与治理协议。

Result: 形成了一套连接智能体演化与动态图拓扑的框架，涵盖四分类体系、九个子领域映射、五种评估与治理协议，为自我演化智能体的设计与治理提供了结构化的分析透镜。

Conclusion: 通过将智能体演化视为动态图变换，可以更紧凑地理解和设计自我演化智能体，动态图学习可作为其可复用基础设施，而图感知的评估与治理协议能够补充端到端任务评估，从而支持更可靠的智能体演化。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based agents are increasingly becoming self-evolving systems that persist across interactions, maintain memories, use tools, acquire skills, refine workflows, and coordinate with other agents. These capabilities make agent states structural and dynamic: entities, relations, attributes, dependencies, and execution structures change with new evidence, feedback, and environmental conditions. Existing graph-agent surveys typically treat graphs as support structures for agent functions rather than as evolving substrates, while self-evolving-agent surveys focus on agent-level mechanisms and rarely discuss graph topology evolution. Thus, the coupling between evolving agent state and dynamic graph topology remains underexplored. This survey connects these two research lines by framing \textit{agent evolution as dynamic graph transformation}. We model agent state as a dynamic graph, where memories, tools, skills, workflows, and inter-agent relations are represented as typed nodes, edges, and subgraphs updated through schema-constrained rewrites. Based on this formulation, we organize existing dynamic-graph-based methods for self-evolving agents into four taxonomies: node/feature evolution, edge/topology evolution, subgraph activation, and cross-component co-evolution. Building on this taxonomy, we propose dynamic graph learning as reusable infrastructure for self-evolving agents and map nine dynamic-graph-learning subfields to agent-evolution capabilities, discussing their adaptations and possible failure modes. Finally, we discuss five types of graph-aware evaluation and governance protocols from a dynamic-graph perspective, which complement end-task evaluation. The goal is to provide a compact structural lens for designing and governing self-evolving agents.

</details>


### [326] [Emergence of Agentic AI: A Review on Evolution, Background, Working Principles, Applications, Adoption Factors, and Future Research Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18110)
*AKM Bahalul Haque,Al Amin Islam Ridoy,Mohammad Rayhan,Ivan Porres*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对Agentic AI进行了系统性综述，涵盖其基本原理、历史演化、架构与功能、跨领域实际应用，并提出了一个包含系统质量维度的利益相关者采纳意图框架，旨在为研究人员和实践者提供全面的理解和未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic AI正在快速发展并有望变革多个领域，因此需要深入理解该技术、调查其最新研究进展，以评估改进和应用的潜在空间，为研究人员和实践者提供有价值的洞见。

Method: 采用系统性综述方法，收集并分析近年在各领域发表的Agentic AI学术贡献，涵盖其基本原理、历史与理论演化、架构工作机理、跨领域实际应用，并通过研究分析提出一个基于系统质量维度的利益相关者采纳与使用意图的综合框架。

Result: 该综述提供了对Agentic AI的全面理解，涵盖其当前发展与应用，识别了关键研究空白，并提出了未来研究方向及一个利益相关者采纳框架。

Conclusion: Agentic AI具有巨大变革潜力，但目前仍存在研究空白和挑战；本文通过系统综述为研究人员和实践者提供了清晰的现状全貌与未来研究路线图。

Abstract: Agentic AI is gaining new insights and advancements in the field of Artificial Intelligence, fostering significant potential to enable rapid transformation across various domains.This rapid advancement and the potential to revolutionize various domains advocate the need for a deeper understanding and firm grasp of the technology. Moreover, an investigation into state of the art research directions in agentic AI needs to be conducted to comprehensively assess the potential scope for improvement and application.Therefore, to address these objectives, a comprehensive review can provide researchers and practitioners with valuable insights into the current state and future research scopes of agentic AI.Hence, this work considers the recently published scholarly contributions in agentic AI across various domains and discusses the fundamentals and working principles of Agentic AI, traces the historical and theoretical evolution of agency in artificial systems, explores and discusses Agentic AIs architecture, working principles, and functionalities, explores real-world applications of Agentic AI across various domains, analyzes the research findings, identifies current challenges, and discuss potential future research directions, and proposes a comprehensive framework of stakeholders intention to use and adopt Agentic AI with the help of proposed system quality dimensions.Therefore, this systematic review provides researchers and practitioners with a comprehensive understanding of Agentic AI, its current developments and applications, highlights key research gaps, and outlines future research directions.

</details>


### [327] [Solving Is Not Drawing: A Benchmark for Diagrammatic Reasoning in Olympiad Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18111)
*Hsien Xin Peng,Anthony Kim,Alvin Li,Calvin Supasanya,Shivank Garg,Kevin Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本论文提出一个开放源码基准测试，用于评估基础模型在奥林匹克几何问题中"绘制图形"的能力，而非仅"求解答案"的能力。基准包含954道自包含的几何问题（其中297道为困难子集），每道题配有解决方案和用Asymptote代码编写的高保真人工图形，并提供文本、代码、图像、VLM和基于约束的五类度量指标。实验发现当前基础模型在绘图方面表现不佳，平均编译成功率仅36.14%，揭示了"数学推理强"不等于"能构建准确几何图形"这一显著能力鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有基准（如MathVista、MathVerse）只评估模型能否得出正确答案，但"解决几何问题"与"绘制问题所依赖的图形"是两种不同的技能。正确解题需要忠实图形、合适的辅助构造和几何关联，而尚无基准单独度量模型构造图形本身的能力，这一关键能力未被测量。

Method: 构建了一个开放源码基准测试，包含954道自包含的奥林匹克几何问题（含297道困难子集），每道题配备解决方案及用可渲染的Asymptote代码编写的人工高保真图形。同时设计了一套涵盖文本、代码、图像、视觉语言模型和基于约束的度量指标的评估体系，用于量化所谓的"图形推理"（diagrammatic reasoning）能力。

Result: 对当前基础模型的评估显示，模型在解题与绘图之间存在着明显差距：它们生成的图形保真度显著较低，平均编译成功率仅为36.14%。这表明即使模型具备强大的数学推理能力，也不能推出其能构建准确的几何图形。

Conclusion: 论文发现数学推理能力与几何图形构造能力并不等价，强推理能力并不意味着能绘制出忠实于原题的准确图形。该基准填补了现有评测的空白，为未来研究和评估基础模型的图形推理能力提供了标准化的测试基础。基准和数据集已公开发布。

Abstract: Foundation models such as GPT and Claude now solve olympiad-level mathematics with remarkable proficiency, so much so that geometry problem solving has become a standard proxy for their mathematical reasoning. Yet solving a geometry problem and drawing the figure it depends on are not the same skill: progress often hinges on a faithful diagram with the right auxiliary constructions and incidences, and it is unclear that a model which reasons its way to the answer can also produce one. A growing collection of benchmarks, including MathVista, and MathVerse, measures whether models reach the correct answer, but to our knowledge, none isolate the distinct ability to construct the diagram itself, leaving this capability unmeasured. We introduce an open-source benchmark that targets this gap: 954 self-contained olympiad geometry problems, with a 297-problem hard subset, each paired with its solution and a human-authored, high-fidelity diagram in renderable Asymptote code, together with a suite of text-, code-, image-, VLM-, and constraint-based metrics for what we term diagrammatic reasoning. Evaluating current foundation models reveals a pronounced gap between solving and drawing: their diagrams are markedly less faithful, with an average compile success rate of only 36.14\%. Strong mathematical reasoning, we find, does not imply the ability to construct accurate geometric diagrams. Our benchmark and dataset can be accessed at https://huggingface.co/datasets/max98765/hard_geometry_problems_with_diagrams.

</details>


### [328] [Position: AI Leaderboards Are Underserving the Global South: A Case Study from India](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18117)
*Sourav Banerjee,Saikat Saha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张AI排行榜在制度设计上结构性不适应全球南方需求，因其缺乏独立治理、利益冲突政策和指标演化机制。问题不在于缺少数据或基准，而在于制度设计——全球排行榜未纳入已有的高质量区域基准（如印度的IndicSUPERB、MILU、LAHAJA，非洲的IrokoBench，阿拉伯语的AlGhafa），且缺乏治理机制强制其纳入。通过对58名AI从业者的咨询研究（以印度为案例），发现从业者一致偏好正式治理和基于披露的冲突管理，提出解决方案是建设从一开始就具备独立治理的区域排行榜，而非更多数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI排行榜在全球南方（特别是印度、非洲、阿拉伯语区）难以发挥作用，核心障碍并非数据或基准的缺失，而是制度设计的缺陷。现有全球排行榜缺乏独立治理、利益冲突政策和指标演化机制，且商业压力只会纠正影响北方付费用户的排行榜失灵，全球南方缺乏同等的制衡力量，导致影响印地语、斯瓦希里语或阿拉伯语使用者的问题被永久性忽略。

Method: 以印度为案例（14亿人口、22种官方语言、已有高质量基准但缺乏可信聚合机制），对58名AI从业者进行咨询研究，收集其对排行榜治理、利益冲突管理的偏好意见，据此评估现有制度缺陷并提出制度性解决方案。

Result: 咨询结果显示58名AI从业者一致偏好正式治理结构和基于披露的冲突管理机制，验证了全球南方排行榜问题的根源在于制度设计而非数据缺失，并确认了建立独立治理的区域排行榜的可行性。

Conclusion: 解决全球南方AI测评问题的关键在于建设从一开始就具备独立治理的区域排行榜，而非单纯增加数据集。应建立具有独立治理、利益冲突管理和指标演化机制的制度化基础设施，以填补全球南方在可信AI测评聚合方面的制度空白。

Abstract: This position paper argues that AI leaderboards are structurally ill-suited to serving the Global South because they lack independent governance, conflict-of-interest policies, and mechanisms for metric evolution.
